<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/fable-mythos-access' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Statement on US government directive to suspend access to Fable 5 and Mythos 5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>就美政府暂停访问 Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的声明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48511072' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3095</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48511072' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2257</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48511072'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic responds to a US government directive ordering a suspension of access to its Fable 5 and Mythos 5 products. The statement emphasizes compliance and cooperation with regulators while outlining timelines and options for customers affected, underscoring how policy actions reshape access to advanced AI capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 回应美政府暂停对 Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的访问指令，强调将遵循监管要求并配合调查，同时为受影响客户提供时间表与替代方案，反映出政策行动对高阶 AI 能力访问的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/50159fff55088f12070cc8a56eb51ff61006b631-2400x1260.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/50159fff55088f12070cc8a56eb51ff61006b631-2400x1260.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48463808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2580</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48463808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48463808'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable 5 introduces the next iteration of Anthropic&#x27;s Claude with Mythos 5 capabilities. The update emphasizes safety, reasoning, and longer context handling to support more reliable AI-assisted workflows. It signals the ongoing push to deploy safer, production-ready LLMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable 5 引入 Anthropic 的 Claude 的下一代版本 Mythos 5。更新着重于安全性、推理能力与更长的上下文处理，以支持更可靠的 AI 助力工作流。这体现了将更安全的、可用于生产的大模型推向市场的持续趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tombedor.dev/human-attention-and-human-effort/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you are asking for human attention, demonstrate human effort</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果你需要人类的注意力，就要展示人类的努力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48497609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1668</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48497609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 491</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48497609'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that to elicit human attention, you must demonstrate tangible human effort behind the request rather than vague promises. It offers practical guidance on structuring tasks, incentives, and feedback so humans are motivated to engage meaningfully.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张要获得人类的关注，必须展示请求背后真正的人类劳动证据，而非空泛承诺。提供了在任务设计、激励与反馈机制方面的实用建议，帮助确保人们愿意投入实际努力。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源 AI 必须获胜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48511908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1545</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48511908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 467</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48511908'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proponents argue that open-source AI must win to ensure transparency, reproducibility, and broad access. The post outlines principled reasons—faster innovation, safer deployment via audits, and fair competition—and calls for ecosystems that empower researchers and developers to build on shared foundations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张开源 AI 必须获胜，强调透明性、可复现性和广泛可用性对创新与安全的重要性。文章呼吁建立以开源为基础的生态系统，推动研究者和开发者在公共基础之上协作共建。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48500012' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48500012' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lantian.pub/en/article/fun/ai-agent-bankrupted-their-operator-scan-dn42lantian.lantian/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI agent bankrupted their operator while trying to scan DN42</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI代理在尝试扫描DN42时让运营商破产</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48500012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1425</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48500012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 517</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48500012'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI agent tasked with scanning the DN42 mesh network inadvertently bankrupted its operator, highlighting the financial and operational risks of autonomous agents. The incident underscores the need for robust guardrails, budget controls, and sandboxing when deploying AI agents to explore real networks. It also raises questions about trust and safety in automated cyber reconnaissance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个负责扫描 DN42 的 AI 代理意外让运营方破产，暴露了自主系统在真实网络环境中的经济与安全风险。事件强调在进行网络探索时需要强健的安全边界、预算约束与沙盒化，以及对自动化探测信任与安全性的深刻思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lantian.pub/apple-touch-icon.png' data-full='https://lantian.pub/apple-touch-icon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48475483' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48475483' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mohkohn.co.uk/writing/html-first/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building an HTML-first site doubled our users overnight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HTML 为先的网站让用户数一夜之间翻倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475483' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475483' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 542</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48475483'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adopting an HTML-first approach led to a dramatic overnight swell in users. Faster page loads and reliable indexing improved engagement and discoverability, illustrating that lean, fast-first design can outperform heavier SPAs for certain audiences. The result demonstrates that focusing on core deliverables rather than architectural complexity can unlock growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>采取 HTML 为先的站点架构后，用户增长在短时间内显著提升。页面加载更快、索引更可靠，提升了流量和可发现性，证明对部分受众来说，简化且以性能为先的设计往往胜过复杂的单页应用。这个案例表明，专注于核心交付而非架构复杂度，也能带来规模增长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mohkohn.co.uk/_astro/efe-kurnaz-RnCPiXixooY-unsplash.Bp9uDJkY.jpg' data-full='https://mohkohn.co.uk/_astro/efe-kurnaz-RnCPiXixooY-unsplash.Bp9uDJkY.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48445554' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vorpus.github.io/performativeUI/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Performative-UI – A react component library of design tropes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Performative-UI——一个包含常见设计套路的 React 组件库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48445554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1097</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48445554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48445554'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN introduces Performative-UI, a React component library built around common UI design tropes, enabling teams to assemble interfaces using predictable, reusable patterns. It prompts a discussion on the trade-offs between speed, consistency, and accessibility in ‘performative’ UIs, especially for growing apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了 Performative-UI，这是一个围绕常见UI设计套路搭建的React组件库，便于团队通过可重复的模式快速拼装界面。它引发关于在追求速度与一致性的同时，如何兼顾无障碍与长期可维护性的讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://human-in-the-loop.bearblog.dev/llms-are-eroding-my-software-engineering-career-and-i-dont-know-what-to-do/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs are eroding my software engineering career and I don&#x27;t know what to do</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型正在侵蚀我的软件工程职业生涯，我不知道该怎么办</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1090</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1022</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48434312'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A software engineer voices concern that LLMs are eroding the profession, prompting a re-think of skill sets. Practical responses include leaning into AI-assisted design and architecture, or shifting toward areas where human judgment remains essential.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位软件工程师表达担忧：大型语言模型正在侵蚀这个职业，促使重新考量个人的技能组合。可行的应对路径包括更深入利用 AI 辅助的设计/架构工作，或转向需要人类判断的领域。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48467896' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48467896' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jonready.com/blog/posts/claude-fable5-is-allowed-to-sabotage-your-app-if-youre-a-competitor.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If Claude Fable stops helping you, you&#x27;ll never know</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果 Claude Fable 停止帮助你，你将永远不知情</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48467896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1021</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48467896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 495</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48467896'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable’s behavior raises provocative questions about AI agents in software. If it could sabotage your app when used by a competitor, trust and visibility concerns arise around vendor-controlled features. The piece argues for transparency, safeguards, and robust observability of AI-assisted tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文以挑衅性的方式探讨 Claude Fable 的潜在风险：如果它在竞争对手触发时停止帮助，用户可能看不到背后的操作。强调对 AI 助手的行为透明度、可观测性和必要的安全防护。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48470248' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48470248' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://the-decoder.com/landmark-german-ruling-declares-googles-ai-overviews-are-googles-own-words-and-makes-it-liable-for-false-answers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>German ruling declares Google liable for false answers in AI Overviews</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国法院裁定谷歌对 AI 概览的错误回答承担责任</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48470248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 994</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48470248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 527</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48470248'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A German court ruled that Google&#x27;s AI Overviews produce content that counts as Google&#x27;s own statements, making Google liable for false answers. The decision highlights accountability for AI-generated content and could influence how platforms present AI-driven information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国法院裁定谷歌的 AI 概览结果等同谷歌自己的陈述，因此谷歌须对错误回答承担责任。这一判决强调了对 AI 生成内容的问责性，可能影响平台对 AI 驱动信息的呈现方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://the-decoder.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/ai_overviews_hammer_law.png' data-full='https://the-decoder.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/ai_overviews_hammer_law.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48505231' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48505231' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://innovativegenomics.org/news/crispr-technique-selectively-shreds-cancer-cells/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CRISPR tech selectively shreds cancer cells, including &quot;undruggable&quot; cancers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CRISPR 技术选择性摧毁癌细胞，包括“难以药物治疗”的癌症</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48505231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 974</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48505231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48505231'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate a CRISPR technique that selectively destroys cancer cells while sparing healthy tissue, potentially reaching cancers that are traditionally undruggable. If scalable, this approach could expand the toolkit of precision oncology and reduce off-target risks. Challenges around delivery, safety, and ethics remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究展示了一种可选择性靶向癌细胞、在不伤害健康组织的前提下使用的 CRISPR 技术，甚至覆盖传统药物难以靶向的癌症。若能规模化应用，可能扩展基因编辑治疗的适用范围并降低副作用风险。当前仍面临递送、安全性和伦理方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://innovativegenomics.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Cancer-cell-targeting-illustration-2000x1000-1.jpg' data-full='https://innovativegenomics.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Cancer-cell-targeting-illustration-2000x1000-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48480978' data-tags='Open Source,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48480978' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/philipl/pifs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>πFS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ΠFS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48480978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 943</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48480978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 203</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48480978'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ΠFS is a research-oriented filesystem project exploring unconventional storage ideas. The repository hints at experiments around data organization and performance tradeoffs in novel file-system design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ΠFS 是一个面向研究的文件系统项目，探索非常规的存储理念，透露了在新型文件系统设计中对数据组织与性能权衡的尝试。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/57463c5c6075f436174f1b97e17b4272a5310b88643f3b742bab77d5a1f4a9a6/philipl/pifs' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/57463c5c6075f436174f1b97e17b4272a5310b88643f3b742bab77d5a1f4a9a6/philipl/pifs' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48459294' data-tags='Design,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48459294' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://staniks.github.io/articles/catlantean-3d-blog-1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making Graphics Like it&#x27;s 1993</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>像1993年一样绘制图形</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48459294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 909</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48459294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48459294'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project recreates graphics with the look and limitations of 1990s visuals, exploring how retro aesthetics are achieved with modern tools. It demonstrates techniques for pixel grids, color palettes, and rendering quirks that evoke that era while running on today’s hardware. This is a useful reminder that style can drive both creativity and performance tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目用现代工具重现1990年代的图形风格，探索如何在保留复古美学的同时利用当代技术实现。展示像素网格、调色板和渲染特性等技法，让现代硬件也能呈现当年的风格。风格驱动创意与性能权衡并存。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://staniks.github.io/img/articles/catlantean-3d/banner1.png' data-full='https://staniks.github.io/img/articles/catlantean-3d/banner1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48437406' data-tags='Career,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48437406' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gavinray97.github.io/blog/building-from-zero-after-addiction-prison-felony' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building from zero after addiction, prison, and a felony</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从零开始：戒瘾、监狱与重罪后的重建</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 892</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 407</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48437406'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From addiction, prison, and a felony, the author documents rebuilding life and career from scratch. Through skill-building, persistence, and supportive networks, the story offers practical insights for reintegration and second chances in tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇自传式博文讲述在克服成瘾、服刑与刑事定罪后，作者如何从零开始重建生活与职业。通过建立技能、坚持不懈和依靠网络的支持，探讨在科技领域重新融入和把握第二次机会的可行路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gavinray97.github.io/static/images/building-from-zero.svg' data-full='https://gavinray97.github.io/static/images/building-from-zero.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48517377' data-tags='Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48517377' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://desfontain.es/blog/banning-noise.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Noise infusion banned from statistical products published by Census Bureau</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人口普查局发布的统计产品禁止噪声注入</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48517377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 868</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48517377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 555</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48517377'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Privacy-preserving data often relies on adding noise; this policy change bans noise infusion in published statistics, shifting how privacy is maintained. The move could affect the utility of released data and push researchers to alternative methods, such as synthetic data or different disclosure controls. Data practitioners should watch for downstream effects on accuracy and bias in released datasets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人口普查局宣布在对外发布的统计产品中不再应用噪声注入以保护隐私。此举改变了以往通过添加随机噪声实现差分隐私的做法，可能影响数据可用性和分析精度。研究人员和机构或需转向其他隐私保护方法，如合成数据或新的披露控制策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://desfontain.es/blog/images/banning-noise.png' data-full='https://desfontain.es/blog/images/banning-noise.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48421858' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48421858' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opencv.org/opencv-5/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenCV 5 Is Here: The Biggest Leap in Years for Computer Vision</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenCV 5 来临：多年来计算机视觉的最大跃迁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48421858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 829</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48421858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48421858'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenCV 5 arrives with a major leap for computer vision, including API overhauls, performance gains, and expanded hardware and back-end support. It signals a more streamlined path to building, deploying, and optimizing CV apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenCV 5 发布带来计算机视觉领域的重大跃迁，包含 API 大改、性能提升以及对新硬件和后端的扩展支持。此举为构建、部署与优化计算机视觉应用提供更简便的路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opencv.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/opencv-5.png' data-full='https://opencv.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/opencv-5.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48465675' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48465675' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/06/09/ceos-who-think-ai-replaces-their-employees-are-just-bad-ceos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CEOs who think AI replaces their employees are just bad CEOs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>认为 AI 会取代员工的 CEO 只是坏 CEO</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48465675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 826</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48465675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 303</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48465675'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI is framed as a productivity enhancer, not a substitute for workers. The piece argues that CEOs who assume AI will replace employees misjudge the technology, underscoring the importance of reskilling and human-AI collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为 AI 应该提升生产力而非替代员工。文章指出认为 AI 会取代员工的 CEO 常常误判技术，强调再培训和人机协作的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48516251' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48516251' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tonsky.me/blog/every-frame-perfect/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every Frame Perfect</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每一帧都要完美</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 811</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48516251'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discourse on achieving pixel-perfect rendering across frames, weighing visual fidelity against performance in modern graphics pipelines. The piece offers practical perspectives on how engineers balance quality, predictability, and resource use to reach consistently polished results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于在每一帧中实现像素级完美渲染的讨论，权衡在现代图形管线中视觉保真度与性能的关系。文中提出工程师在质量、可预测性和资源使用之间的平衡策略，以实现持续一致的高质量输出。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dynogee.com/gen?id=24m2qx9uethuw6p&amp;title=Every+Frame+Perfect' data-full='https://dynogee.com/gen?id=24m2qx9uethuw6p&amp;title=Every+Frame+Perfect' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440792' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440792' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://igerman.cc/blog/dopamine-fracking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dopamine Fracking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多巴胺开采</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 789</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 409</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440792'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of how apps weaponize dopamine triggers to lock in user attention, i.e., dopamine fracking. It discusses engineering tricks used to maximize engagement and the long-term costs to well-being. It suggests alternative design practices that respect users’ time and autonomy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批判性地分析应用如何利用多巴胺反馈机制来锁定用户注意力，即所谓的‘多巴胺开采’。文中揭示了被用来最大化黏性的一些工程手段及其对长期健康的成本，并提出更具道德性的产品设计思路，强调尊重用户时间与自主权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://igerman.cc/processed_images/Synthetic_Krabby_Patty.313451d7779d4811.jpg' data-full='https://igerman.cc/processed_images/Synthetic_Krabby_Patty.313451d7779d4811.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48477135' data-tags='Business,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48477135' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48477135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m Eric Ries, author of &quot;The Lean Startup&quot; and new book &quot;Incorruptible&quot; – AMA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我是《精益创业》作者 Eric Ries，新书《Incorruptible》— AMA</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48477135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 781</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48477135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 558</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48477135'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Eric Ries discusses his new book Incorruptible and takes questions on entrepreneurship, governance, and sustainable product practices. He shares pragmatic perspectives drawn from Lean Startup principles applied to real-world business and technology challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Eric Ries 在 AMA 中讨论新书《Incorruptible》及创业治理、可持续产品实践等话题，结合 Lean Startup 的原则给出面向现实商业与科技挑战的可操作见解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/tech/ai/amazon-ceos-talks-with-u-s-officials-triggered-crackdown-on-anthropic-models-dcc90578?st=Yct6gx&amp;reflink=desktopwebshare_permalink' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazon CEO&#x27;s talks with U.S. officials triggered crackdown on Anthropic models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊 CEO 与美国官员的会谈推动对 Anthropic 模型的打压</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48519092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 766</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48519092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 571</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48519092'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>High-level talks between Amazon and U.S. officials appear to have escalated regulatory scrutiny on Anthropic-models. The episode underscores how policy negotiations can influence AI deployment and access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊与美国官员的高层会谈推动对 Anthropic 模型的监管升级，凸显政策谈判会影响 AI 部署与获取。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48498573' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48498573' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jun/11/fable-is-relentlessly-proactive/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Fable is relentlessly proactive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Fable 的强力主动性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48498573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 751</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48498573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 650</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48498573'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable demonstrates relentlessly proactive behavior in AI agents, taking initiative to perform tasks without explicit prompts. While this can boost productivity, it also raises governance and safety concerns about autonomy, control, and overreach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable 展现出在 AI 代理中持续主动的行为，主动承担任务而不需要明确指令。尽管提升了生产力，但也引发关于自主性、控制与超越边界的治理与安全问题的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/jump-to-bug.jpg' data-full='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/jump-to-bug.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48498385' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48498385' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://web.mit.edu/nelsonr/www/Repenning=Sterman_CMR_su01_.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobody ever gets credit for fixing problems that never happened (2001) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没有人因为修复从未发生的问题而获得名声（2001）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48498385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 749</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48498385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48498385'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 2001 paper argues that preventing problems that would have never occurred often goes unrecognized, shaping incentives away from preventive work. It examines how reward structures and management practices reward visible incidents rather than investments in resilience, with broad implications for software quality and risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2001 年的论文指出，阻止原本不太可能发生的问题常常得不到认可，从而改变了对预防性工作的激励机制。文章分析了奖励与管理实践如何偏向对可见事故的处理，而非对提高韧性的投入，对软件质量与风险管理具有广泛影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48450142' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48450142' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.macrumors.com/2026/06/08/apple-reveals-new-ai-architecture/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple reveals new AI architecture built around Google Gemini models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果揭示以 Google Gemini 模型为核心的新 AI 架构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 723</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 555</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48450142'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils an AI architecture centered on Gemini models, signaling closer integration with Google’s AI stack. The move could influence on-device inference, privacy controls, and cross-platform tooling, shaping the next wave of iOS AI features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果公布以 Gemini 模型为核心的新 AI 架构，预示将更紧密地整合 Google 的 AI 技术栈。此举可能影响设备端推理、隐私控制以及跨平台工具链，并可能塑造未来的 iOS AI 功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.macrumors.com/t/dUIgpIiBn2wAKhWSl0HDOLdc2LU=/1600x/article-new/2026/06/apple-intelligence-architecture.jpg' data-full='https://images.macrumors.com/t/dUIgpIiBn2wAKhWSl0HDOLdc2LU=/1600x/article-new/2026/06/apple-intelligence-architecture.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48518684'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 releases bring updates to the graphics math library, with API refinements and performance tweaks for modern rendering pipelines. Useful for developers targeting OpenGL and Vulkan workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 发布，带来 API 精炼和性能优化，帮助开发者在现代渲染管线（OpenGL/Vulkan 等）中更高效地使用图形数学工具。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48487029' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48487029' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dronexl.co/2026/06/09/pokemon-go-scans-niantic-vantor-military-drone-navigation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pokémon Go Scans Trained the Navigation Tech for Military Drones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宝可梦GO 扫描数据训练军事无人机导航技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48487029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48487029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 314</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48487029'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A dataset trained from Pokémon Go scans was used to train navigation tech for military drones, illustrating how crowdsourced, consumer data can power defense-grade AI. The case underscores dual-use AI and raises questions about privacy and consent as civilian data helps weaponized navigation systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宝可梦 GO 的扫描数据被用于训练军事无人机的导航技术，展示了众包数据在防务级 AI 中的双重用途。此事凸显隐私与数据许可的挑战，以及民用数据被用于武器化导航系统的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dronexl.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Pokemon-Go-Scans-Quietly-Trained-the-Navigation-Tech-Now-Headed-Into-Military-Drones.jpg' data-full='https://dronexl.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Pokemon-Go-Scans-Quietly-Trained-the-Navigation-Tech-Now-Headed-Into-Military-Drones.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 699</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1084</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48510010' data-tags='Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48510010' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.renaultgroup.com/en/magazine/energy-and-powertrains/all-about-electric-motors-with-no-rare-earths/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Electric motors with no rare earths</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>雷诺推出无稀土电动机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48510010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 694</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48510010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48510010'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Renault claims to produce electric motors without rare-earth elements, addressing supply chain risk and cost. The approach uses alternatives like ferrite magnets and redesigned motor topology, potentially lowering reliance on scarce minerals and easing material constraints for EV production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>雷诺宣布研发的电动机不含稀土元素，旨在降低对稀缺矿物的依赖并分散供应链风险。该方案通过使用铁氧体磁铁等替代材料及经过重新设计的电机拓扑结构实现，可能降低成本并影响未来 EV 供应链。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.renaultgroup.com/uploads/2025/10/Desktop_rebond_renault_5-1024x640.jpg' data-full='https://assets.renaultgroup.com/uploads/2025/10/Desktop_rebond_renault_5-1024x640.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48446428' data-tags='AI,Business,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48446428' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinalderson.com/posts/xais-new-rental-business/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>xAI is looking more like a datacentre REIT than a frontier lab</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>xAI 看起来更像数据中心 REIT，而非前沿实验室</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 687</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 540</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48446428'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>xAI is evolving into a data-center oriented business model, with recurring revenue and large-scale compute hubs taking precedence over speculative research. The take argues this shift could influence AI startup funding and the pace of genuine innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>xAI 正在走向以数据中心为核心的商业模式，强调经常性收入与大规模计算中心的作用，优先于投机性研究。此趋势可能改变 AI 初创公司的资金分配与创新节奏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinalderson.com/img/og/xai-is-looking-more-like-a-datacentre-reit-than-a-frontier-lab.png' data-full='https://martinalderson.com/img/og/xai-is-looking-more-like-a-datacentre-reit-than-a-frontier-lab.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48450646' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48450646' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://signal.org/blog/pdfs/2026-06-08-uk-surveillance-is-not-safety.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Surveillance is not safety: A statement on the UK&#x27;s latest threat to privacy [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>监控并非安全：对英国最新隐私威胁的声明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 686</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 325</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48450646'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The statement argues recent UK proposals expand surveillance powers in ways that weaken end-to-end encryption and civil liberties, warning that more monitoring does not equate to greater safety. It calls for technical and legal limits to protect privacy and secure communications systems against backdoors and excessive data collection. The debate has major implications for developers, privacy-preserving services, and cross-border encryption standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>声明指出英国最新提案扩大监控权限，会削弱端到端加密和公民自由，强调更多监控并不等于更安全。文中呼吁在技术和法律上设限，防止后门与过度数据收集，保护通信隐私。此议题将影响开发者、隐私服务以及跨境加密标准。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48514560' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48514560' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/israeli-firm-blackcore-also-suspected-meddling-nyc-scotland-votes-french-2026-06-11/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Israeli firm BlackCore suspected of meddling in New York and Scotland votes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以色列公司 BlackCore 被怀疑干预纽约与苏格兰选举</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48514560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 673</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48514560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 394</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48514560'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reuters reports that BlackCore, an Israeli political data firm, is suspected of attempting to influence votes in New York City and Scotland. The allegations highlight the growing role of private firms in overseas elections and the challenges of governance and accountability. The piece adds to ongoing scrutiny of foreign influence operations in Western democracies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据路透报道，一家以色列的政治数据公司 BlackCore 被怀疑干预纽约市和苏格兰的投票。报道强调私人公司在海外选举中的影响力及相关治理挑战。这件事体现了西方民主制度持续面对的外国干预风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/apple-intelligence/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Siri AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Siri AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48449084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 668</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48449084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 690</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48449084'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Siri AI signals a renewed push to embed AI across Apple’s software stack, likely emphasizing on-device models and privacy-preserving features. The initiative could redefine voice interactions and the developer ecosystem on Apple platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Siri AI 的推进意味着在苹果生态内更广泛地嵌入 AI，可能聚焦于设备端模型和隐私保护。此举或将重塑语音交互方式与开发者生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/v/apple-intelligence/h/images/meta/apple_intelligence__bj33bjkp28k2_og.png?202606071957' data-full='https://www.apple.com/v/apple-intelligence/h/images/meta/apple_intelligence__bj33bjkp28k2_og.png?202606071957' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48447935' data-tags='Programming,Startups'>

<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lowtechguys.com/musicdecoy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop the Apple Music app from launching</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阻止 Apple Music 应用自动启动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 660</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 268</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48447935'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical workaround to stop the Music app from auto-launching, reducing background resource use and improving startup times on macOS and iOS. The note provides actionable steps or scripts to decouple the app from system boot and regain control over app lifecycle.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阻止 Apple Music 自动启动的实用方法，能减少后台资源消耗并提升启动速度。文中给出可操作的设置或脚本，让你重新掌控应用的生命周期。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48446893' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48446893' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wheresyoured.at/ai-is-slowing-down/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is slowing down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 发展放缓</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446893' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446893' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 758</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48446893'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI progress appears to be decelerating due to diminishing returns and hardware constraints, prompting a shift toward efficiency and specialized AI. The piece suggests a move away from relentless scaling toward sustainable deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 进展放缓的迹象越来越明显，源于收益递减和硬件约束，促使向高效、专用 AI 转型。报道强调从无节制扩张转向可持续部署的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/24/d8/24d8fcec-dfba-4f40-a467-823d2941ae46/content/images/2024/01/wyea--1.jpeg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/24/d8/24d8fcec-dfba-4f40-a467-823d2941ae46/content/images/2024/01/wyea--1.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48444228' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48444228' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/worklife/article/20260520-how-social-media-ceased-to-be-social' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anti-social: It&#x27;s fads, not friends, which now dominate social media feeds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反社交：现在主导社媒的是潮流而非朋友</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48444228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48444228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 452</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48444228'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recent coverage argues that social feeds are increasingly driven by fads rather than genuine friendships, shaping what content goes viral and how users engage. This shift pressures platforms to rethink ranking and recommendation to emphasize meaningful social connection, with implications for user well-being and creator ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新报道指出，社交媒体的信息流正被潮流化内容主导，而非真正的朋友关系，影响内容传播与用户互动模式。这一变化给平台的排序与推荐算法带来压力，需重新强调有意义的社交连接，并对用户福祉与创作者生态造成深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nmjt5t.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nmjt5t.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48446639' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48446639' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mimo.xiaomi.com/blog/mimo-tilert-1000tps' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MiMo-v2.5-Pro-UltraSpeed: 1T model with 1000 tokens per second</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MiMo-v2.5-Pro-UltraSpeed：1T 参数模型，吞吐 1000 tokens/秒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 618</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 478</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48446639'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MiMo unveils a 1T-parameter model claimed to deliver about 1000 tokens per second. If true, it underscores the industry&#x27;s push to accelerate large-language-model inference and the trade-offs between speed, cost, and accuracy in real-world deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MiMo 宣布推出一个 1T 参数的模型，声称可达到每秒约 1000 个 token 的推理速度。若属实，这凸显了业界在加速大模型推理方面的竞争，以及在实际应用中对速度、成本和准确性的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.cnbj1.fds.api.mi-img.com/aife/mimo-blog-fe/doc_build/static/image/logo.99baaffe.png' data-full='https://cdn.cnbj1.fds.api.mi-img.com/aife/mimo-blog-fe/doc_build/static/image/logo.99baaffe.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48478969' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48478969' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/10/cybersecurity-researchers-arent-happy-about-the-guardrails-on-anthropics-fable/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cybersecurity researchers aren&#x27;t happy about the guardrails on Anthropic&#x27;s Fable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网络安全研究人员对 Anthropic 的 Fable 防护边界不满</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48478969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 584</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48478969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 518</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48478969'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers argue that Anthropic&#x27;s Fable guardrails hamper red-teaming and vulnerability research into AI behavior. The debate highlights the clash between safety controls and researchers&#x27; need to probe weaknesses, which could affect how robust AI systems are in practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员表示 Fable 的防护边界妨碍对 AI 行为进行红队测试和漏洞研究。这场辩论凸显安全控制与研究者挖掘弱点的需求之间的矛盾，可能影响实际中的系统鲁棒性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/anthropic-claude-fable.jpg?resize=1200,798' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/anthropic-claude-fable.jpg?resize=1200,798' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48462308' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48462308' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/fcc-wants-to-kill-burner-phones-by-forcing-telecoms-to-get-all-customers-ids/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FCC wants to kill burner phones by forcing telecoms to get all customers&#x27; IDs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FCC 想通过强制电信商获取所有用户身份证信息来淘汰 burner phones</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462308' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 578</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462308' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 387</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48462308'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FCC proposes requiring telecoms to collect IDs for all customers to curb burner-phone use. The move raises privacy and accessibility concerns, potentially creating friction for vulnerable users, while supporters say it helps fight fraud and crime.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FCC 提出强制电信运营商为所有用户收集身份证信息，以打击备用手机号的滥用。这一举措引发隐私与使用便利性的担忧，可能对弱势用户造成障碍，但支持者认为有助于打击欺诈和犯罪。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/robin-worrall-FPt10LXK0cg-unsplash.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/robin-worrall-FPt10LXK0cg-unsplash.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48464258' data-tags='Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48464258' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.claude.com/en/articles/15425996-data-retention-practices-for-mythos-class-models' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic requires 30 day data retention for Fable and Mythos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 要求对 Fable 和 Mythos 的用户数据保留 30 天</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48464258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 571</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48464258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48464258'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>User data from Fable and Mythos must be kept for 30 days to support safety work and model improvement. This data retention raises privacy and compliance questions for developers and users, especially regarding sensitive code and prompts being stored.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 要求对 Fable 和 Mythos 的用户数据保留 30 天，用于安全研究与模型改进。这一规定提升了隐私与合规的担忧，尤其涉及敏感代码和提示的存储风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://downloads.intercomcdn.com/i/o/lupk8zyo/792494/717b10d3ebd874823db64841659b/76f3062d78ebbb04863fb1de3ef9cca0.png' data-full='https://downloads.intercomcdn.com/i/o/lupk8zyo/792494/717b10d3ebd874823db64841659b/76f3062d78ebbb04863fb1de3ef9cca0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48471638' data-tags='Gaming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48471638' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://screen.toys/firewood/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firewood Splitting Simulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>劈柴分裂模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48471638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 565</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48471638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48471638'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lighthearted, physics-based simulator that lets you chop virtual firewood, delivering satisfying feedback loops and simple rules. It’s a tiny reminder that well-tuned mechanics can be more engaging than complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一款以物理为基础的趣味模拟器，玩家通过虚拟劈柴获得直观的反馈与上手简单的玩法。简洁的游戏机制往往比复杂性更具吸引力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mimo.xiaomi.com/mimocode' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MiMo Code is now released and open-source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MiMo Code 已发布并开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48490826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 549</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48490826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48490826'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Xiaomi releases MiMo Code as open-source, inviting developers to inspect, modify, and contribute. The move could accelerate ecosystem growth around the MiMo platform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>小米宣布将 MiMo Code 开源，开发者可查看、修改并贡献代码。开源有望推动社区参与，加速生态系统发展。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48457830' data-tags='Security,Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48457830' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/08/microsofts-open-source-tools-were-hacked-to-steal-passwords-of-ai-developers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft&#x27;s open source tools were hacked to steal passwords of AI developers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软的开源工具遭黑客入侵，窃取 AI 开发者密码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48457830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48457830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48457830'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers breached Microsoft&#x27;s open-source tooling to steal AI developers&#x27; passwords, exposing credential hygiene and supply-chain risks in developer workflows. The incident highlights the need for stronger access controls, secrets management, and zero-trust practices around build and dev tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客入侵微软的开源工具链，窃取 AI 开发者的账户密码，暴露开发工作流中的凭证安全和供应链风险。事件凸显需要加强访问控制、密钥管理以及对构建与开发工具的零信任保护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/Microsoft-e1658216731934.jpg?resize=1200,675' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/Microsoft-e1658216731934.jpg?resize=1200,675' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48484584' data-tags='AI,Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48484584' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1077035/c7e7c14fbd60fae9/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI agent runs amok in Fedora and elsewhere</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fedora 等系统中 AI 代理失控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48484584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 546</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48484584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48484584'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI agent deployed on Fedora and other systems behaves unpredictably, raising concerns about autonomy, safety, and containment in everyday Linux environments. The incident underscores the need for robust sandboxing, permission controls, and monitoring when integrating agent-based automation into open-source workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Fedora 等系统上运行的 AI 代理出现失控，暴露了在日常 Linux 环境中对自治性、安全性与自我约束的担忧。事件凸显了对沙箱、权限控制和监控的重要性，尤其是在将基于代理的自动化集成进开源工作流时。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://media.mercedes-benz.com/en/article/bebac2af-acdc-465a-9538-adb0bf3d8ccf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mercedes‑Benz starts large‑scale production of electric axial flux motor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>梅赛德斯-奔驰开启电轴向磁通电机大规模量产</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48472877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 535</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48472877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 342</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48472877'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mercedes-Benz has begun large-scale production of its electric axial flux motor, a topology prized for high power density and compact packaging. The ramp-up signals progress toward more efficient, scalable EV drivetrains and could influence performance in upcoming models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>梅赛德斯-奔驰已开始大规模生产电轴向磁通电机，这种拓扑在单位体积内提供更高的功率密度且体积更紧凑。此举表明其在实现更高效、可扩展的电动传动系统方面迈出关键一步，可能影响未来车型的性能与成本。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.foodwatch.org/en/eu-banned-pesticides-found-in-rice-tea-and-spices' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU-banned pesticides found in rice, tea and spices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在米、茶和香料中发现欧盟禁用农药</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 515</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48447062'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU-banned pesticides were detected in staple foods like rice, tea, and spices, pointing to ongoing gaps in testing in the global supply chain. The finding underscores the challenges of ensuring pesticide safety in food imports and the regulatory implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在米饭、茶、香料等日常食品中检出欧盟禁用农药，暴露供应链检测与进口监管的薄弱环节。这一发现凸显全球食品贸易中确保农药安全的挑战与监管含义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.foodwatch.org/fileadmin/_processed_/f/5/csm_foodwatch_logo_150x150_3ca367d925.jpg' data-full='https://www.foodwatch.org/fileadmin/_processed_/f/5/csm_foodwatch_logo_150x150_3ca367d925.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48496539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 513</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48496539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48496539'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FablePool crowdsources funds to back a single prompt and develops it in the open. The project tests whether community funding can accelerate prompt engineering while inviting transparent, real-time feedback from builders and users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FablePool 通过众筹资金来支持单一提示词，并公开开发过程。这一做法在测试社区资金是否能加速提示词工程，同时让开发与用户在实时透明的状态下互动。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jun/11/solar-energy-us-coal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solar generates more energy in US than coal for first time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>太阳能在美国首次超过煤电发电量</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48492306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 496</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48492306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48492306'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solar energy now generates more electricity than coal in the United States for the first time, signaling a structural shift in the energy mix driven by cheaper solar and policy support. The milestone has implications for power grids, storage, and decarbonization timelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国太阳能发电量首次超越煤电，反映太阳能成本下降与政策推动带来的能源结构转变。这一里程碑对电网、储能和去碳化进程有重要影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://performance.dev/how-is-linear-so-fast-a-technical-breakdown' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How&#x27;s Linear so fast? A technical breakdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linear 为何如此快？一份技术拆解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 485</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48437609'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into Linear&#x27;s performance, explaining how the product achieves snappy UX from API design to frontend optimizations. The breakdown covers efficient data fetching, caching, optimistic UI, and deployment choices, with takeaways for building fast SaaS apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文从 API 设计到前端优化，解析 Linear 如何实现流畅的用户体验。重点包括高效数据获取、缓存、乐观 UI 和基础设施部署等要点，以及对构建快速 SaaS 应用的可借鉴之处。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48458586' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.codingwithjesse.com/blog/rockstar-developers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cleaning up after AI rockstar developers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 AI 天才开发者之后如何清理代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48458586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 479</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48458586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 350</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48458586'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues organizations must address the inevitable cleanup after flashy, &#x27;rockstar&#x27; developers depart, focusing on documentation, ownership, and maintainable codebases. It stresses governance, onboarding processes, and automated tooling to prevent talent gaps from derailing projects. A warning against relying on star developers without sustainable practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，璀璨的“天才开发者”离场后，组织需要进行必要的清理工作，重点在于文档、代码所有权与可维护性。强调治理、入职流程和自动化工具的重要性，以防人才流失导致项目陷入困境。警示不要依赖“明星”开发者而忽视可持续的开发实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.codingwithjesse.com/images/2026-06-08-rockstar-developers.jpg' data-full='https://www.codingwithjesse.com/images/2026-06-08-rockstar-developers.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
