<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48513536' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://12gramsofcarbon.com/p/tech-things-there-is-a-massive-shadow' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There is a shadow hanging over this Fable thing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>这桩 Fable 事件笼罩着阴影</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48513536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 476</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48513536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 472</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48513536'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues there are unresolved risks around Fable, including regulatory pressure, potential safety or governance concerns, and opaque sourcing. It warns that policy and reputational factors could affect adoption and ecosystem trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出关于 Fable 存在尚未解决的风险，如监管压力、安全与治理担忧，以及来源不透明等问题。强调政策与声誉因素可能影响采纳与生态信任，呼吁更清晰的治理与透明度。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/ai-artificial-intelligence/948280/anthropic-claude-fable-invisible-distillation-guardrail' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic apologizes for invisible Claude Fable guardrails</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 474</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 413</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic acknowledges issues with invisible guardrails in Claude Fable and issues a public apology. The incident highlights the tension between safety guardrails and usability in conversational AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 承认 Claude Fable 的隐性护栏存在问题并公开道歉。这一事件凸显了安全护栏与可用性之间在对话式人工智能中的矛盾。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/changelog/2026-06-09-upcoming-breaking-changes-for-npm-v12/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Upcoming breaking changes for npm v12</a></p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48481126' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/farmer-donates-land-for-a-park-city-sells-it-for-data-center-development-usd10-gift-became-usd10m-for-city-government-with-usd30m-tax-expected-over-next-decade' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Farmer donates land for a park, city sells it for $10M as data center land</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>农民捐地建公园，市政府以1000万美元出售作数据中心用地</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48481126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 472</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48481126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48481126'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A farmer donated land for a park, and the city subsequently sold it for $10M to be used as data center land. The deal is projected to generate about $30M in tax revenue over the next decade, highlighting the fiscal and policy tradeoffs when public land is repurposed for tech infrastructure. It underscores how data-center demand can reshape local land values and public finances.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名农民捐出土地用于公园，市政府随后以1000万美元出售给数据中心开发商以作数据中心用地。未来十年税收预计约3000万美元，这揭示了在公共土地被用于科技基础设施时的财政与政策取舍。此事凸显数据中心需求如何重塑地方土地价值与财政状况。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/iRypPh9tngoWdjn9byB345-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/iRypPh9tngoWdjn9byB345-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.yacnews.com/albania-is-not-for-sale-kushners-4-billion-resort-triggers-flamingo-revolution-asset-freeze-and-an-eu-warning/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Albania Is Not for Sale: Kushner&#x27;s $4B Resort Triggers&#x27;Flamingo Revolution&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿尔巴尼亚不卖地：库什纳4亿美元度假村引发‘火烈鸟革命’</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48461012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 449</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48461012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48461012'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A $4 billion Kushner-backed resort project in Albania has sparked protests and a government asset freeze, drawing warnings from the EU. The saga highlights how large luxury developments can confront political resistance, regulatory scrutiny, and international concerns over investment and influence. It shows how real estate diplomacy can become a flashpoint in small nations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在阿尔巴尼亚，一座由库什纳投资的40亿美元度假村项目引发示威并导致政府资产被冻结，同时遭到欧盟的警告。这起事件凸显豪华开发在政治抵制、监管审查与国际投资关切面前的脆弱性，以及地产外交在小型国家可能成为冲突焦点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/a-farmer-donated-land-to-turn-into-a-park-the-city-is-building-a-massive-data-center-instead/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Farmer Donated Land to Turn into a Park. The City Is Building a Data Center</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>农民捐地建公园 城市却在此地兴建数据中心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 439</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48446439'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An entire parcel donated for a public park is being repurposed for a massive data center. The decision highlights tensions between green space preservation and growing digital infrastructure, with implications for energy use and community impact. It raises questions about governance, transparency, and the value of public space in land deals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一块为公园捐出的土地正被改建成大型数据中心。此举揭示绿色空间保护与日益增长的数字基础设施之间的矛盾，以及对能源消耗和社区影响的潜在后果。涉及治理透明度与土地交易中公共空间价值的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48502347' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48502347' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K2.7-Code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi K2.7-Code: open-source coding model with better token efficiency</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi K2.7-Code：开源编码模型，令牌效率更高</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48502347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 438</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48502347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48502347'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kimi K2.7-Code is an open-source coding model optimized for token efficiency, enabling more cost-effective code generation and longer prompts. By improving token usage, it aims to close the gap with proprietary models while staying transparent and community-driven. The release underscores a growing trend toward more efficient, open-source AI tooling for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi K2.7-Code 是一个强调高令牌利用率的开源编码模型，降低代码生成的成本并支持更长的输入。它在提升效率的同时保持透明与社区驱动，回应了开源 AI 工具日益增长的需求。此举反映了开发者生态中对更高性价比模型的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/moonshotai/Kimi-K2.7-Code.png' data-full='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/moonshotai/Kimi-K2.7-Code.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48442075' data-tags='Science,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48442075' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reeserichardson.blog/2026/05/28/how-much-of-thermo-fishers-antibody-data-has-been-manipulated/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How much of Thermo Fisher&#x27;s antibody data has been manipulated?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Thermo Fisher 的抗体数据被操纵？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48442075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 431</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48442075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48442075'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post alleges that a significant share of Thermo Fisher’s antibody data may have been manipulated, raising concerns about data integrity, reproducibility, and trust in biotech datasets. It argues that industry-scale data manipulation would have serious implications for research, diagnostics, and investor confidence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指称 Thermo Fisher 的抗体数据可能存在大量操控，引发对数据完整性、可重复性以及生物技术数据集信任度的担忧。若属实，这种规模的数据操纵将对科研、诊断和投资者信心产生严重影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/reeserichardson.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/thermo_fisher_wb_featured_image.png?fit=1200%2C574&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/reeserichardson.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/thermo_fisher_wb_featured_image.png?fit=1200%2C574&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48479452' data-tags='Programming,AI,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48479452' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/29045' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Desktop spawns 1.8 GB Hyper-V VM on every launch, even for chat-only use</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Desktop 启动时会开启一个 1.8 GB 的 Hyper-V 虚拟机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 430</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48479452'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Desktop launches a 1.8 GB Hyper-V virtual machine on every start, even when users only chat. The heavyweight virtualization increases startup time and resource use, raising questions about desktop AI app design and user experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Desktop 启动时会开启一个 1.8 GB 的 Hyper-V 虚拟机，即使仅用于聊天也如此。这种重型虚拟化增加了启动时间和资源消耗，引发对桌面 AI 应用设计与用户体验的质疑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7548f25cbb7f27292f39867bc10f65e857bb330323d7af42dd896e91fc853d87/anthropics/claude-code/issues/29045' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7548f25cbb7f27292f39867bc10f65e857bb330323d7af42dd896e91fc853d87/anthropics/claude-code/issues/29045' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48495928' data-tags='Security,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48495928' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/jsrailton/status/2064661778978533571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malware developers added nuclear and biological weapons text to to their spyware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>恶意软件开发者在其间谍软件中加入了关于核生物武器的文本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48495928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 428</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48495928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 228</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48495928'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report notes malware authors embedded text about nuclear and biological weapons inside spyware samples. The move underscores how attackers package sensational content or noise to misdirect analysis and complicate detection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道指出恶意软件作者在间谍软件样本中嵌入关于核生物武器的文本，凸显攻击者如何通过夸张信息混淆分析、增加检测难度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HKclElRWMAATMuP.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HKclElRWMAATMuP.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48463024' data-tags='Politics,Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48463024' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/apple-failed-make-its-ai-tool-comply-eu-regulations-eu-commission-says-2026-06-09/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple decided not to roll out Siri in EU after denied request for exemption</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果因豁免申请被否，决定在欧盟不推出 Siri</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48463024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 428</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48463024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 693</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48463024'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple has decided not to roll out Siri in the European Union after its exemption request was denied. The move highlights how current EU AI and privacy rules constrain big tech deployments in Europe, even as they press ahead elsewhere. The decision reflects ongoing regulatory friction shaping product strategy for voice assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果在豁免申请被拒后，决定不在欧盟推出 Siri。此举凸显欧盟对AI与隐私规则对大型科技公司在欧洲部署的制约，即使在其他地区仍在推进相关功能。此事显示监管压力正在影响语音助手的产品策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48453275' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48453275' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsencrypt.org/documents/LE-SA-v1.7-June-04-2026-diff.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt bans certificate usage in any US sanctioned territory [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Let’s Encrypt 禁止在任何美国制裁辖区使用证书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48453275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 428</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48453275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 354</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48453275'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Let’s Encrypt updates policy to ban certificate usage in any U.S.-sanctioned territory, reflecting a tougher enforcement stance. The change affects developers and operators relying on the free TLS certificates in affected regions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Let’s Encrypt 更新政策，禁止在任何美国制裁辖区使用证书，体现了更严格的执法趋势。此举将影响在相关地区依赖免费证书的开发者与运营商。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48449187' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48449187' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48449187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: What are tools you have made for yourself since the advent of AI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：自从 AI 兴起后你为自己做了哪些工具？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48449187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 423</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48449187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 723</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48449187'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Community members share a variety of personal tools they&#x27;ve built post-AI: prompt libraries, local model runners, data-cleaning scripts, task automation wrappers, and integrations that streamline workflows. The responses highlight pragmatic, small-scale engineering—glue code and utilities—that often deliver the most day-to-day productivity gains. The thread is a useful source of real-world patterns for leveraging AI practically rather than as research projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社区用户列举了自 AI 兴起以来自制的工具：提示库、本地模型运行器、数据清理脚本、自动化包装器及各种集成，旨在简化工作流。回复显示，常常是那些务实的小工程（胶水代码与工具）在日常工作中带来最大生产力提升。该讨论为如何把 AI 实用化提供了现实范例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48489402' data-tags='Programming,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48489402' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://curlewis.co.nz/posts/lines-of-code-got-a-better-publicist/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lines of code got a better publicist</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代码行数获得更好的公关</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489402' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 417</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489402' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48489402'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lines of code are being championed more than before, but the metric remains a poor proxy for software quality. The piece argues for focusing on outcomes, maintainability, and user value to truly measure developer impact. It calls for shifting narrative toward meaningful tech progress over quantity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>代码行数被吹捧为衡量标准，但它依然不能有效反映软件质量。文章主张应以产出、可维护性和用户价值为核心来衡量开发者的影响，推动叙事从数量转向实际的技术进步。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://curlewis.co.nz/posts/lines-of-code-got-a-better-publicist/hero_hu_8251bd8edd692786.jpg' data-full='https://curlewis.co.nz/posts/lines-of-code-got-a-better-publicist/hero_hu_8251bd8edd692786.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48509182' data-tags='Legal,Data,Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48509182' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/7ffcace7-9dc0-4e7e-9912-895ac073f979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Palantir loses legal challenge against Swiss investigative magazine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>帕兰蒂尔在瑞士调查杂志诉讼中败诉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48509182' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 413</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48509182' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48509182'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Palantir loses a legal challenge against a Swiss investigative magazine, ceding a case that could affect how data analytics firms interact with journalism and data sourcing. The ruling may influence how digital surveillance and data contracts are scrutinized by the press.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帕兰蒂尔在针对一家瑞士调查性杂志的法律诉讼中败诉，可能影响数据分析公司与新闻机构在数据来源与合作上的边界。此裁决或将影响对数字监控、数据合约以及新闻调查的监督方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F83a75134-cd80-4b47-9cf8-299260f9e4e8.jpg?source=next-barrier-page' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F83a75134-cd80-4b47-9cf8-299260f9e4e8.jpg?source=next-barrier-page' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48473166' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48473166' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48473166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AWS Bedrock to require sharing data with Anthropic for Mythos and future models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AWS Bedrock 将要求客户将数据分享给 Anthropic，用于 Mythos 与未来模型的训练</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48473166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 410</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48473166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48473166'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AWS Bedrock will require customers to share data with Anthropic to train Mythos and future models. This data-sharing policy ties customer inputs to model improvements and raises questions about privacy, governance, and data control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AWS Bedrock 将要求客户将数据分享给 Anthropic，用于 Mythos 及未来模型的训练。这一数据共享政策将使模型改进与客户数据之间绑定，同时引发隐私、治理与数据控制方面的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48517199' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48517199' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://economist.com/science-and-technology/2026/06/12/treating-pancreatic-tumours-may-have-revealed-cancers-master-switch' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New pancreatic cancer drug might open the door to much longer survival times</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新型胰腺癌药物或将显著延长生存期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48517199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 403</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48517199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48517199'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Preliminary results suggest the new pancreatic cancer drug could significantly extend survival, offering a potential paradigm shift for a disease with few effective options. If confirmed in larger trials, it could redefine treatment approaches and spur development of similar targeted therapies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>初步数据表明，这款新型胰腺癌药物可能显著延长患者生存期，这在治疗这类高致死性癌症中算是罕见的突破。若后续试验证实，其有望推动对胰腺癌治疗策略的重新定位，并刺激开发更多针对性治疗。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48471970' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48471970' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neowin.net/news/google-chrome-is-killing-all-ublock-origin-bypasses-microsoft-edge-opera-to-follow/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chrome is looking to permanently drop MV2 extension</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chrome 计划永久弃用 MV2 扩展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48471970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 402</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48471970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 419</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48471970'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Chrome is moving to permanently drop MV2 extensions in favor of MV3, changing how extensions operate. The shift will affect popular add-ons and could restrict some bypass capabilities, impacting developers and power users. It highlights the ongoing security-performance trade-offs in browser extension ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 将永久弃用 MV2 扩展，转向 MV3 的生态。此举可能影响广泛使用的扩展功能，甚至限制部分绕过能力，影响开发者与高阶用户。此举凸显浏览器扩展生态在安全、性能之间的持续权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.neowin.com/news/images/uploaded/2025/03/1741346735_ublock_origin_in_chrome_story.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.neowin.com/news/images/uploaded/2025/03/1741346735_ublock_origin_in_chrome_story.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48492210' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.endorlabs.com/learn/claude-fable-5-mythos-grade-hype' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Fable 5: mid-tier results on coding tasks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Fable 5：编码任务上的中等水平表现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48492210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 402</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48492210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48492210'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable 5 shows mid-level performance on coding tasks, signaling that current LLM-based coding assistants still struggle with robust correctness and nuanced debugging. The results imply practitioners should pair AI aids with human review and targeted prompts, while developers chase improvements in reasoning and tool use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable 5 在编码任务上呈现中等水平表现，表明现有的基于大型语言模型的编码助手在正确性和细节调试方面仍有不足。结果提示开发者应将 AI 辅助与人工复核、针对性提示结合使用，同时推进推理与工具使用方面的改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6574c9e538a34feac8cec013/6a2a386899570e6f8d646e19_new%20model%20claude.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6574c9e538a34feac8cec013/6a2a386899570e6f8d646e19_new%20model%20claude.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ioccc.org/2025/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 29th International Obfuscated C Code Contest (IOCCC) 2025 Winners</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>第29届国际混淆C代码竞赛（IOCCC）2025 获奖作品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48432199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 396</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48432199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48432199'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IOCCC 2025 winners showcase the year&#x27;s boldest obfuscated C programs, pushing compilers and tricks to the extreme. Entries rely on preprocessor gymnastics, clever data encodings, and unusual memory tricks to fit a program into tiny form factors. They illustrate C&#x27;s expressive boundaries while underscoring the importance of readability in real-world code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年 IOCCC 获奖作品展现了年度最具创造性的混淆 C 代码，常用预处理器花招、另类数据表示和内存布局技巧。此类作品凸显 C 的灵活性，同时也提醒在实际软件开发中注重可读性与可维护性的重要性，并展示数十年演进后的仍具实现空间的编程模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ioccc.org/png/hamburger-icon-open.png' data-full='https://www.ioccc.org/png/hamburger-icon-open.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48527700' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gabrielweinberg.com/p/people-are-consuming-ai-like-they' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Not everyone is using AI for everything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>并非人人都把 AI 用到极致</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 430</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48527700'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece challenges AI fever by arguing most people are not using AI for everything; adoption remains selective and task-specific. It emphasizes realistic expectations and practical limits of AI in everyday life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章对 AI 热潮提出质疑，表示大多数人并未把 AI 用来做所有事情，应用仍然是有选择性的。强调对 AI 的实际价值与局限要有清晰判断。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!n_r-!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F957dc207-33f4-4e66-a821-66f340c9a623_1424x1404.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!n_r-!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F957dc207-33f4-4e66-a821-66f340c9a623_1424x1404.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://runtimewire.com/article/deepseek-v4-pro-beats-gpt-5-5-pro-on-precision' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek V4 Pro beats GPT-5.5 Pro on precision</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440448'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek V4 Pro claims higher precision than GPT-5.5 Pro in benchmark tasks, signaling improvements in dedicated AI tooling. The claim invites scrutiny of evaluation methods and raises questions about when proprietary vs open models trade accuracy for safety and speed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek V4 Pro 在基准测试中声称在精度上超越 GPT-5.5 Pro，显示在专用工具上的改进。这一说法需要审视评测方法，并引发关于专有与开源模型在准确性、安全性与性能之间权衡的问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/devenjarvis/lathe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Lathe – Use LLMs to learn a new domain, not skip past it</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48433756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 371</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48433756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48433756'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lathe uses LLMs to thoroughly learn a new domain rather than skimming it, enabling deeper understanding and reproducible workflows. This approach highlights how AI can augment genuine knowledge acquisition, not just search or prompt tailoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lathe 通过大模型深入学习一个新领域，而不是走马观花，从而实现更深的理解和可重复的工作流。这显示了 AI 能力在真正的知识获取方面的潜力，而不仅仅是搜索或提示微调。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48448012' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48448012' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/08/massachusetts-votes-to-pass-new-privacy-rights-bill-that-bans-sale-of-precise-location-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Massachusetts bans sale of precise location data in new privacy rights bill</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马萨诸塞州通过新隐私法禁止出售精确定位数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48448012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48448012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48448012'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Massachusetts adopts a privacy bill banning the sale of precise location data, extending protections for consumers. The law could reshape ad-tech practices, data brokerage, and app telemetry that relies on fine-grained geolocation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马萨诸塞州通过的新隐私法禁止出售精确定位数据，扩展了对消费者的保护。这将影响广告技术、数据经纪和依赖精准地理位置的应用，推动更透明的数据处理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/GettyImages-1392356345.jpg?resize=1200,800' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/GettyImages-1392356345.jpg?resize=1200,800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48442725' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48442725' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cypherpunkbooks.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Cypherpunk Library</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cypherpunk 图书馆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48442725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48442725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48442725'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Cypherpunk Library curates a collection of cypherpunk writings and related materials that trace privacy, cryptography, and decentralized technologies across decades. It serves as a valuable resource for researchers and practitioners seeking historical context and practical insights into how code and policy intersect in digital civil liberties.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该档案汇集了大量 Cypherpunk 派系的著作与相关资料，展示隐私、密码学与去中心化技术的演变。它为研究者和从业者提供理解数字公民自由在技术与治理交汇处的历史与实践洞见。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.cypherpunkbooks.com/opengraph-image.png?opengraph-image.0uxdxnxa5mj37.png?dpl=dpl_GPGJH42ANc1s8FuaQi83mZUN9rMp' data-full='https://www.cypherpunkbooks.com/opengraph-image.png?opengraph-image.0uxdxnxa5mj37.png?dpl=dpl_GPGJH42ANc1s8FuaQi83mZUN9rMp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48464140' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48464140' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.oneusefulthing.org/p/what-it-feels-like-to-work-with-mythos' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What it feels like to work with Mythos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>与 Mythos 一起工作是什么感觉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48464140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 361</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48464140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 317</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48464140'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>What it&#x27;s like to work with Mythos reveals the day-to-day realities of collaborating with an AI teammate. It offers a candid look at the human-AI dynamic in product development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>与 Mythos 一同工作的真实感受揭示了与 AI 同事协作的日常现场。文章强调调试趣事、安全约束，以及团队在构建可信任 AI 软件时的迭代过程。它提供了关于产品开发中人机协作关系的直观观察。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!hxhY!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff58114c7-7286-4a9f-ab75-3c8a7e414dc3_2382x1247.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!hxhY!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff58114c7-7286-4a9f-ab75-3c8a7e414dc3_2382x1247.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48449665' data-tags='AI,Programming,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48449665' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.apple.com/documentation/coreai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Core AI Framework</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apple Core AI 框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48449665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 361</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48449665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48449665'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s Core AI framework provides a unified API for running and integrating on-device machine learning and generative models, emphasizing performance and privacy. It aims to simplify model deployment across Apple platforms with optimizations for hardware like Apple silicon and tooling for developers to leverage local inference. The framework signals Apple&#x27;s push to make advanced AI features native while keeping data on-device.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apple 的 Core AI 框架为在设备上运行和集成机器学习及生成式模型提供统一接口，重点在性能与隐私。它简化了模型在 Apple 平台上的部署，针对 Apple 芯片做出优化并为开发者提供本地推理的工具。该框架表明 Apple 力求将高级 AI 功能原生化，同时尽量在设备端保留数据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://docs.developer.apple.com/tutorials/developer-og.jpg' data-full='https://docs.developer.apple.com/tutorials/developer-og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48452317' data-tags='Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48452317' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/openai-submits-confidential-s-1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Confidential submission of draft S-1 to the SEC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>向 SEC 提交 S-1 初稿的保密提交</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48452317' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 359</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48452317' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 312</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48452317'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI has confidentially submitted its S-1 draft to the SEC, signaling readiness for a potential public offering while keeping details private for now. This move usually precedes a formal filing and public timeline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 已向 SEC 保密提交 S-1 初稿，表示为可能的公开发行做准备，同时先行保护细节。此举通常是正式备案和公开时间表的前奏，待公司决定推进时再披露。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/1kKjVqt4LdJo6DAWO4rklb/93d7ae2d6a5cb70b17fd5ff440552291/confidential-submission-of-draft-s-1-to-the-sec-16x9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/1kKjVqt4LdJo6DAWO4rklb/93d7ae2d6a5cb70b17fd5ff440552291/confidential-submission-of-draft-s-1-to-the-sec-16x9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48520807' data-tags='AI,Legal,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48520807' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.sky.com/story/derbyshire-police-officer-investigated-for-using-ai-to-create-evidence-in-multiple-cases-13553661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Police officer investigated for using AI to &#x27;create evidence&#x27; in multiple cases</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>警官因在多起案件中使用 AI &#x27;制造证据&#x27; 而接受调查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48520807' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 347</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48520807' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48520807'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Derbyshire police officer is under investigation for allegedly using AI to generate or &#x27;create&#x27; evidence in multiple cases. The investigation raises red flags about the reliability and admissibility of AI-assisted forensics, and could prompt tighter controls on how law enforcement uses AI in investigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德比郡警方一名警官因涉嫌在多起案件中使用 AI 生成“证据”而接受调查。这一事件凸显了 AI 辅助取证的可靠性与证据可采性问题，或促使对执法部门使用 AI 的流程和监管加强。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48523992' data-tags='Programming,Web,Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48523992' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sqltoerdiagram.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Free SQL→ER diagram tool, runs in the browser, nothing uploaded</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>浏览器端免费 SQL→ER 图工具，数据不上传</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48523992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 336</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48523992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48523992'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser-based tool that converts SQL schemas into ER diagrams without sending data to the cloud. It helps developers visualize database structures locally, with options to export diagrams for documentation. The emphasis is privacy and accessibility for quick modeling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个在浏览器中运行的工具，可将 SQL 架构转成 ER 图且不会把数据上传到云端。帮助开发者在本地直观地可视化数据库结构，并可导出图示用于文档。强调隐私和快速建模的便捷性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sqltoerdiagram.com/og.png' data-full='https://sqltoerdiagram.com/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48496396' data-tags='Culture,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48496396' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mayrhofer.eu.org/post/leaving-google/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why I&#x27;m Forced to Say Farewell: Google Management Has Lost Its Moral Compass</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何我被迫离开：谷歌管理层已失去道德指引</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48496396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48496396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 259</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48496396'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Former Google employee explains that moral direction in management has eroded, driving their departure. He criticizes leadership choices that privilege growth and optics over users and employees, signaling broader concerns about governance in tech giants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者以亲身经历揭露谷歌管理层的道德方向正在走偏，促使他选择离职。文中批评某些领导决策优先考虑增长和表象，而忽视用户与员工的福祉，反映科技巨头治理与信任的广泛担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mayrhofer.eu.org/post/leaving-google/featured.jpg' data-full='https://www.mayrhofer.eu.org/post/leaving-google/featured.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48504697' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48504697' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.lopp.net/call-to-action-stop-the-fcc-kyc-regime/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Call to Action: Stop the FCC&#x27;s KYC Regime</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>行动号召：阻止FCC的KYC制度</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48504697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48504697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48504697'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues against the FCC&#x27;s proposed Know-Your-Customer regime, warning that it would mandate broad identity verification across communications and expand privacy and civil-liberties risks. It outlines why technologists should care and offers concrete avenues to push back, including policy comments and privacy-preserving options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章呼吁反对FCC拟议的KYC制度，认为会强制对通信服务进行身份认证，扩大隐私与公民自由风险。作者同时指出科技从业者应如何进行政策干预，并提出保护隐私的替代方案与行动方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.lopp.net/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/fcc_kyc.jpg' data-full='https://blog.lopp.net/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/fcc_kyc.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48458890' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48458890' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/luke8086/gentleos32' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GentleOS – Classic operating system with a lovely retro GUI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GentleOS——带复古 GUI 的经典操作系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48458890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48458890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48458890'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GentleOS is a hobbyist OS that recreates a classic desktop feel with a charming retro GUI. It showcases how lightweight systems can still provide a productive developer experience while leaning into nostalgia; a potential teaching platform for OS fundamentals and UI design. It&#x27;s a reminder that well-crafted UIs can coexist with modern abstractions in niche projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GentleOS 是一个爱好者层面的操作系统，带有迷人的复古GUI，重现经典桌面风格。它展示了轻量级系统如何在保持怀旧感的同时提供高效的开发体验，亦可作为操作系统基础和界面设计的教学平台。精心设计的界面仍能在小众项目中与现代抽象共存。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1edf6125b6e70a01271a87f446a064538dd893fbe733610a18c20004b510456d/luke8086/gentleos32' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1edf6125b6e70a01271a87f446a064538dd893fbe733610a18c20004b510456d/luke8086/gentleos32' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48529990' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48529990' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tamnd/kage' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Kage – Shadow any website to a single binary for offline viewing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Kage — 将任意网站打包为离线可用的单一二进制文件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48529990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48529990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48529990'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kage bundles a website into a single standalone binary, enabling offline viewing without a browser or network. It makes distribution and archiving of a site trivial for demos, edge devices, or offline access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kage 将任意网站打包成一个独立的可执行文件，离线也能浏览。提高了便携性，适合离线演示、备份与边缘部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f83b8cc479704de4a3c8bbe1bcfd5d564f74bba6dcf58631b629015428c9bc88/tamnd/kage' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f83b8cc479704de4a3c8bbe1bcfd5d564f74bba6dcf58631b629015428c9bc88/tamnd/kage' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48478471' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/diffusion-gemma-faster-text-generation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DiffusionGemma: 4x Faster Text Generation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DiffusionGemma：文本生成提速约4倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48478471' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48478471' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48478471'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DiffusionGemma introduces a diffusion-based optimization that speeds up text generation by roughly four times. The acceleration reduces latency and compute costs for interactive AI tasks, enabling more responsive applications. This reflects a broader push to make large models practical at interactive scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DiffusionGemma 通过扩散式优化实现约4倍的文本生成提速，显著降低交互式 AI 场景的延迟与算力成本。此举让大模型在实时应用中更具可用性，体现了提升交互性规模化的重要方向。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>与未实现的梦想和解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48437290'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Making Peace with Your Unlived Dreams explores accepting unpursued paths while focusing energy on meaningful goals. It encourages reframing setbacks as part of growth and using intentional choices to move forward. A mindful take on ambition and contentment in tech life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇关于与尚未实现的梦想和解的反思文章，倡导承认未实现的可能性并把精力放在真正重要的目标上。文中提供将挫折视为成长过程的一部分的思考与建议。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48447806' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48447806' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gitdot.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Gitdot – A better GitHub. Open-source, written in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Gitdot — 更好的 GitHub，开源且用 Rust 编写</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447806' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447806' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48447806'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gitdot is an open-source, self-hostable GitHub alternative written in Rust that focuses on performance and simplicity. It aims to offer familiar collaboration features while giving teams control over data and deployment, appealing to organizations wary of centralized platforms. Rust implementation promises speed and safety for large repositories and many concurrent users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gitdot 是一个用 Rust 编写的开源、自托管的 GitHub 替代方案，强调性能与简洁性。它提供熟悉的协作功能，同时让团队掌控数据与部署，适合对集中式平台持保留态度的组织。Rust 的实现有助于在大仓库和高并发场景中提升速度与稳定性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gitdot.io/gitdot-long-black.svg?dpl=dpl_8t5VFhVE6K7TBrthov6GwELeHBLj' data-full='https://gitdot.io/gitdot-long-black.svg?dpl=dpl_8t5VFhVE6K7TBrthov6GwELeHBLj' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48480852' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48480852' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://samwilkinson.io/posts/2026-06-09-anthropics-model-naming-extrapolated' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic&#x27;s model naming, extrapolated</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 模型命名的外推分析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48480852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48480852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48480852'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sam Wilkinson extrapolates Anthropic&#x27;s model naming to infer capabilities and release patterns, suggesting that prefixes may encode performance tiers and alignment safeguards. This approach helps practitioners anticipate features and risks when evaluating new models. A naming taxonomy can aid budgeting and deployment decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者通过对 Anthropic 模型命名的外推，试图从命名模式推断模型能力分级与对齐机制。该分析为评估新模型时提供了对潜在功能与风险的线索，有助于在预算与部署上做出更明智的判断。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A remote code execution vulnerability in AMD software/firmware remains unpatched, renewing questions about disclosure timelines and vendor accountability. The case underscores risks to users and the challenges of hardware security governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 软件/固件中的远程代码执行漏洞尚未修补，引发对披露时机和厂商问责的讨论。此案例凸显用户风险以及硬件安全治理的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>macOS 27 beta breaks the ability to boot Asahi Linux on Apple Silicon, complicating dual-boot setups and open-source Linux on Macs. The compatibility hiccup highlights the fragility of cross-platform open-source efforts.</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48450059' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.admin.ch/en/sustainability-initiative' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Switzerland wil have a referendum to cap population at 10M</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞士将就人口上限定为 1000 万举行公投</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 314</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 807</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48450059'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Swiss referendum aims to cap population at 10 million, a demographic policy with potential implications for housing, immigration, and public services. The move could test how radical population controls are received in a wealthy, small-country context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞士计划就将人口上限设为 1000 万展开公投，涉及住房、移民与公共服务等议题。这个提案在这个富裕、人口规模较小的国家引发对人口政策与经济社会目标之间权衡的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daringfireball.net/2026/06/macos_27_golden_gate_removes_the_dumb_icons_from_menu_items' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sweet Jeebus, macOS 27 Golden Gate Removes the Dumb Icons from Menu Items</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48487435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 313</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48487435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 163</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48487435'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>macOS 27 Golden Gate drops icons from menu items to declutter the UI, leaning into text-first menus. The change fuels debate about visual cues, accessibility, and whether fewer icons speeds up or slows down user recognition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>macOS 27 Golden Gate 将菜单项中的图标移除，转向文本优先的界面设计。这一改动引发关于视觉提示、可访问性以及减少图标是否提升或降低识别速度的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48492533' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zed.dev/blog/introducing-deltadb' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Software is made between commits</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件是在提交之间构建</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48492533' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 311</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48492533' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48492533'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeltaDB introduces a software model where changes are designed and tested between commits, enabling more fluid collaboration and finer-grained versioning. It challenges traditional commit-centric workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeltaDB 提出在提交之间设计与测试变更的软体模型，提升协作的流畅性与版本颗粒度，挑战传统以提交为中心的工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.zed.dev/blog/deltadb-early-access/thumbnail.webp' data-full='https://images.zed.dev/blog/deltadb-early-access/thumbnail.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48515336' data-tags='Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48515336' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.google/blog/a-low-carbon-computing-platform-from-your-retired-phones/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A low-carbon computing platform from your retired phones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>来自退役手机的低碳计算平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48515336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48515336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48515336'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers propose a computing platform that reuses retired smartphones to run workloads, cutting carbon emissions from new device production. The approach leverages edge computing and hardware reuse to offer scalable, energy-efficient compute at low cost. If adopted widely, it could complement data centers with greener options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员提出一套将退役手机重新用于计算任务的平台，以减少新设备生产带来的碳排放。该方法利用边缘计算与硬件再利用，提供低成本、可扩展且更节能的计算能力。若广泛应用，或成为数据中心的绿色替代方案之一。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-research2023-media/images/HO_previewImage1.width-800.format-jpeg.jpg' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-research2023-media/images/HO_previewImage1.width-800.format-jpeg.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48516379' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48516379' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Arch-Linux-AUR-More-Than-1500' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Arch Linux Now Believes Malware Incident Under Control: More Than 1,500 Packages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Arch Linux 认为恶意软件事件已基本控制：超过1500个软件包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48516379'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Arch Linux project reports the AUR malware incident is under control after a broad cleanup. With more than 1,500 affected packages, the episode underscores the fragility of community-maintained ecosystems and the ongoing need for rapid containment, vetting, and package revocation practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Arch Linux 表示 AUR 恶意软件事件已基本得到控制，已进行大规模清理。涉及超过1500个软件包的情况突显了社区维护的生态系统易受影响的现状，以及对快速处置、包签名与撤回等做法的持续需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=cachyos-ubuntu-2510-f43&amp;image=cachyos_nov_1' data-full='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=cachyos-ubuntu-2510-f43&amp;image=cachyos_nov_1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48510341' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://putt.day/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Putt.day a daily mini golf game</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Putt.day——每日迷你高尔夫游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48510341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 304</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48510341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48510341'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tiny, distraction-free browser game that offers a new golf course every day, emphasizing accessibility and delightful visuals. The project showcases what a focused, small-scope app can deliver in user engagement and a fun daily ritual.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个简小的浏览器日常高尔夫游戏，每天随机生成新关卡，强调易用性与清新美术风格。展示了以极小范围也能创造黏性用户体验和每日乐趣的力量。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440064' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440064' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ranpara.net/posts/perceptron-explained-from-scratch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Smallest Brain You Can Build: A Perceptron in Python</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你能构建的最小大脑：用 Python 实现感知机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 303</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440064'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From-scratch, it implements a single-layer perceptron in Python, covering weights, bias, and the basic learning rule. The write-up emphasizes intuition and practical code you can run to see how simple linear classifiers learn. A handy primer for getting hands-on with neural networks before moving to deeper models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从零开始用 Python 实现一个简单的感知机，讲解权重、偏置和学习规则等核心概念。该文注重直观理解，给出可运行的代码，帮助你直观感受线性分类器的学习过程。是入门神经网络的实用入门材料。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ranpara.net/images/your-thumbnail-image.jpg' data-full='https://ranpara.net/images/your-thumbnail-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
