<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48431981' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.janestreet.com/i-design-with-claude-code-more-than-figma-now-index/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I design with Claude more than Figma now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我现在用 Claude 设计多于 Figma</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48431981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 303</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48431981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 261</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48431981'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jane Street’s design workflow increasingly relies on Claude to ideate and iterate designs, surpassing traditional Figma reliance. The author notes Claude’s reasoning and code-assisted capabilities speed up layout and prototype work, though human oversight remains essential for visual polish.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jane Street 的设计流程越来越多地借助 Claude 来构思和迭代设计，甚至超过了对 Figma 的依赖。文章指出在文本驱动设计与原型实现中，Claude 的推理与代码能力能显著提速，但仍需人工把控视觉细节。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.janestreet.com/i-design-with-claude-code-more-than-figma-now-index/figma-to-claude-hero.png' data-full='https://blog.janestreet.com/i-design-with-claude-code-more-than-figma-now-index/figma-to-claude-hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48457181' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48457181' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbc.ca/news/canada/facebook-overseas-alberta-separtism-9.7223966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Facebook is paying people overseas promoting Alberta separatism</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Facebook 向海外人员推广阿尔伯塔分离主义的付费行为</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48457181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48457181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48457181'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Facebook paid overseas promoters to spread Alberta separatism, shedding light on how paid political campaigns and cross-border influence operate on social platforms. The piece raises questions about moderation, transparency, and foreign influence in online discourse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道揭示 Facebook 向海外人员支付报酬来推广阿尔伯塔省分离主义内容，折射出社媒平台上付费政治宣传与跨境影响力的运作。此事引发对平台内容审核、透明度及外国干预的担忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48487540' data-tags='AI,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48487540' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.normaltech.ai/p/why-ai-hasnt-replaced-software-engineers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why AI hasn&#x27;t replaced software engineers, and won&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 AI 尚未取代软件工程师，也不会取代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48487540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48487540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 341</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48487540'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An argument that AI will augment rather than replace software engineers, due to needs for architectural judgment, debugging, and cross-functional collaboration. AI will be a productivity tool and co-pilot, not a wholesale substitution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为 AI 将更多地起到辅助作用而非替代软件工程师，因为需要架构判断、调试能力和跨职能协作。AI 将成为生产力工具和协作者，而非全面替代。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!eBfS!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F931ce348-4a6a-44e9-ad78-322dd8421bd7_1286x764.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!eBfS!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F931ce348-4a6a-44e9-ad78-322dd8421bd7_1286x764.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440952' data-tags='Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440952' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.troyhunt.com/1000-data-breaches-later-the-disclosure-lag-is-worse-than-ever/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1k Data Breaches Later, the Disclosure Lag Is Worse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>经历千起数据泄露后，披露延迟更严重</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440952'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of 1,000 breaches shows the disclosure lag is worse than ever, delaying risk awareness and response. The lengthy gap undermines timely remediation and transparent risk communication. The takeaway: invest in faster detection, real-time telemetry, and stronger breach notification practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对1000起数据泄露的分析显示披露延迟比以往更长，延缓风险识别与应对。长时间的间隔削弱了及时修复和透明的风险沟通。核心要点是加强快速检测、实时遥测，以及更强的泄露通知机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/fb/33/fb3391dc-723d-4e74-b95a-d641b5feb38e/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/photo_2026-05-31_20-34-34.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/fb/33/fb3391dc-723d-4e74-b95a-d641b5feb38e/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/photo_2026-05-31_20-34-34.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48481857' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48481857' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.adafruit.com/product/6125?src=raspberrypi' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Raspberry Pi 5 – 16GB RAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>树莓派 5 搭载 16GB RAM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48481857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48481857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 327</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48481857'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raspberry Pi 5 ships with 16GB of RAM, offering a meaningful uplift for desktop and hobbyist projects. The boost enables more memory-intensive workloads, smoother multitasking, and better headless server use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>树莓派 5 配备 16GB RAM，相比前代带来显著提升，适合桌面应用、爱好者项目和轻量级服务器。更充裕的内存让多任务更流畅、对内存密集型工作更从容。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.adafruit.com/images/480x360/6125-00.jpg' data-full='https://www.adafruit.com/images/480x360/6125-00.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48447598' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48447598' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tonedear.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ear Training Practice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>耳朵训练练习</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48447598'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tone Dear curates structured ear-training drills to improve pitch recognition, interval sense, and rhythm. The exercises help musicians build a reliable internal reference and speed up practice, sight-singing, and improvisation. A practical resource for students and hobbyists alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tone Dear 收集了结构化的耳朵训练练习，提升音高辨识、音程感知和节奏感。练习有助于建立稳定的内部音高参考，提升视唱与即兴表现的速度。对学生与业余爱好者都是实用的工具。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48479852' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48479852' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://geolibre.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GeoLibre 1.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GeoLibre 1.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48479852'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GeoLibre 1.0 marks the first release of an open-source geospatial project focused on privacy-respecting, interoperable mapping tools. The project aims to democratize access to geospatial data and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GeoLibre 1.0 发布，标志着一个强调隐私友好与互操作的开源地理空间项目的初次版本，目标是让地理数据与工具的获取更具普及性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://geolibre.app/assets/geolibre-icon.png' data-full='https://geolibre.app/assets/geolibre-icon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48500447' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48500447' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discourse.ifin.network/t/400-aur-packages-compromised-with-infostealer-and-rootkit/577' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AUR packages compromised with Infostealer and Rootkit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AUR 软件包遭入侵：信息窃取器与 Rootkit</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48500447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 295</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48500447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48500447'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Several AUR packages were compromised to include an infostealer and rootkit payload, enabling data theft and stealthy persistence. This incident underscores supply-chain risks in community-maintained ecosystems and reinforces the need for provenance checks, package signing, and rapid remediation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>若干 AUR 软件包被篡改，捆绑了信息窃取器与 rootkit，能够窃取凭证并实现长期隐蔽运行。事件暴露了社区维护的生态系统的供应链风险，强调加强包签名与来源可追溯性的重要性，以及快速清理受影响包的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://discourse.ifin.network/uploads/default/original/2X/e/efd08247e0471547bbde75ec94dd7598a6683bff.png' data-full='https://discourse.ifin.network/uploads/default/original/2X/e/efd08247e0471547bbde75ec94dd7598a6683bff.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48452001' data-tags='Hardware,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48452001' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/handheld-gaming/developer-gets-half-life-running-at-30-fps-on-a-2007-nokia-n95' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Developer gets Half-Life running at 30 FPS on a Nokia N95</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开发者在诺基亚 N95 上让 Half-Life 以 30 FPS 运行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48452001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48452001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48452001'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer makes Half-Life run at 30 FPS on a Nokia N95, a vivid demonstration of retro hardware porting and optimization. The feat highlights how constraints can inspire clever engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者让《半条命》在诺基亚 N95 上以约 30 FPS 运行，生动展示了对老设备的端口与优化。此成就体现了约束如何激发巧妙的工程实现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/5GpHGZRJr9ojBLYomT6JQQ-1920-80.png' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/5GpHGZRJr9ojBLYomT6JQQ-1920-80.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48510046' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48510046' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://depthfirst.com/research/21-zero-days-in-ffmpeg' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Twenty One Zero-Days in FFmpeg</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FFmpeg 存在二十一处零日漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48510046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 284</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48510046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 193</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48510046'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A detailed analysis discloses 21 zero-day vulnerabilities in FFmpeg, underscoring security risks in widely used multimedia tooling. The findings emphasize the need for prompt patching, responsible disclosure, and robust hardening of multimedia pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>详细分析揭示 FFmpeg 存在二十一处零日漏洞，凸显了广泛使用的多媒体工具链的安全风险。研究强调应尽快打补丁、保持负责任的披露流程，并对多媒体处理管线进行更强的防御。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://depthfirst.com/uploads/21%20zero%20days%20in%20FFmpeg.png' data-full='https://depthfirst.com/uploads/21%20zero%20days%20in%20FFmpeg.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48465269' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48465269' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://naokishibuya.github.io/blog/2022-12-30-gpt-2-2019/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-2: Too Dangerous To Release (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-2：2019 年因太危险而未公开发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48465269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 284</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48465269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48465269'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In 2019, OpenAI withheld the full GPT-2 model citing risks of misuse, opting for a staged release and extensive safety evaluations. The decision highlighted early debates about responsible AI deployment and helped shape norms around disclosing capabilities and governance in the AI community. It also foreshadowed moves toward model cards and more transparent releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在2019年，OpenAI 因担忧滥用风险而没有公开释放完整的 GPT-2 模型，选择分阶段发布并进行安全评估。此决定凸显了对负责任部署的早期辩论，帮助塑造了AI界在披露能力和治理框架方面的共识。它也预示了日后关于模型卡片和更透明发布的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://naokishibuya.github.io/blog/2022-12-30-gpt-2-2019/images/thumbnail.png' data-full='https://naokishibuya.github.io/blog/2022-12-30-gpt-2-2019/images/thumbnail.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48468766' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48468766' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.oscars.dev/posts/rip-software-hackathons-long-live-the-hardware-hackathon/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RIP software hackathons. Long live the hardware hackathon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件黑客松告别，硬件黑客松长存</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48468766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 281</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48468766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48468766'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Software hackathons are fading while hardware-focused events gain relevance. The piece argues hardware hackathons deliver tangible, build-ready outcomes and better reflect real-world constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>软件黑客松逐渐式微，硬件黑客松的价值越发凸显。文中认为硬件黑客松更容易产出可落地的成果，并更贴近现实约束。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.oscars.dev/meta-img.png' data-full='https://blog.oscars.dev/meta-img.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48466277' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48466277' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aarushgupta.io/posts/kan-fpga/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ultrafast machine learning on FPGAs via Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络在 FPGA 上的超快 ML</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48466277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 281</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48466277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48466277'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks enable ultrafast ML on FPGAs by blending a mathematical network design with reconfigurable hardware. This approach promises low-latency inference for edge and data-center workloads, challenging traditional CPU/GPU bottlenecks. It points to a future where specialized hardware accelerates complex models at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络通过将数学网络结构与可重配置硬件（FPGA）结合，实现超快的机器学习推理。这种方法有望为边缘和数据中心工作负载提供低延迟推断，挑战传统的 CPU/GPU 瓶颈。它指向一个用专用硬件在大规模场景加速复杂模型的未来。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://aarushgupta.io/assets/img/LUT_Quantized.png' data-full='https://aarushgupta.io/assets/img/LUT_Quantized.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440463' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440463' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/new-drug-functionally-cures-many-hepatitis-b-virus-infections?user_id=66c4bf745d78644b3aa57b08' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New drug &#x27;functionally cures&#x27; many hepatitis B virus infections</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新药能在多数乙肝感染者身上实现功能性治愈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 280</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440463'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study reports a drug that functionally cures many hepatitis B infections, achieving sustained viral control after treatment in a substantial portion of patients. Functionally cured patients can discontinue therapy while maintaining low viral activity, representing a potential paradigm shift in HBV treatment. Still, larger trials and long-term follow-up are needed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究报道一种新药能在相当数量的乙型肝炎病毒感染者身上实现功能性治愈，治疗后可维持长期病毒控制。功能性治愈意味着在停止治疗后仍能保持低水平的病毒活性，可能改变乙肝治疗的格局。然而，仍需在更大规模的试验和长期随访中验证。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48490057' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48490057' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.businessinsider.com/botsitting-ai-hidden-human-labor-at-work-2026-6' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Workers are spending over 6 hours a week botsitting AI, fueling job frustration</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>员工每周花6小时以上为 AI “护航”，加剧工作挫败感</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48490057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 278</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48490057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 220</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48490057'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Workers are spending over six hours weekly babysitting AI, correcting outputs and monitoring systems. This hidden labor increases burnout and undermines productivity gains from AI adoption. Companies should invest in better tooling, guardrails, and workflows to reduce this friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>员工每周为 AI 系统“保驾护航”超过6小时，纠错与监控输出，增加了工作压力并削弱了 AI 带来的生产力提升。企业应投资更好的工具、规范与工作流，以降低这部分隐性劳动的摩擦。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.insider.com/6a2a9528457accd9f7cea838?width=1200&amp;format=jpeg' data-full='https://i.insider.com/6a2a9528457accd9f7cea838?width=1200&amp;format=jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48477551' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48477551' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bls.gov/news.release/cpi.nr0.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Consumer Price Index up 4.2%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国 CPI 上涨 4.2%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48477551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 274</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48477551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48477551'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The US Consumer Price Index rose 4.2%, signaling inflation remains elevated but has cooled from earlier peaks. The figure informs macro policy, pricing strategies, and tech budgeting as companies plan for wages, costs, and consumer demand. For developers, it translates into more careful cost management and forecasting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国消费者物价指数同比上涨4.2%，表明通胀仍偏高但已从峰值回落。该数字影响宏观政策、定价策略及科技公司的预算规划，企业在薪资、成本与需求预测方面需更加审慎。对开发者而言，意味着更谨慎的成本管理与前景预测。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48507282' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48507282' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.miguelgrinberg.com/post/i-am-not-a-reverse-centaur' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Am Not a Reverse Centaur</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不是反向半人马</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48507282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 271</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48507282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48507282'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the human role in AI-assisted software development, arguing against surrendering judgment to AI and away from ‘reverse centaur’ dynamics where tools override human agency. Provides guidance on designing and using AI copilots with clear human oversight and responsibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文讨论 AI 辅助编程中的人类角色，反对让 AI 完全主导判断，拒绝成为“反向半人马”。提出在设计与使用 AI 辅助工具时应保持明确的人类监督和责任边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.miguelgrinberg.com/static/cards/i-am-not-a-reverse-centaur.jpg' data-full='https://blog.miguelgrinberg.com/static/cards/i-am-not-a-reverse-centaur.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48479705' data-tags='Science,Space,Health,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48479705' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/curiosity-rover-jpl-mars-science' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How JPL keeps the 13-year-old Curiosity rover doing science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JPL 如何让 13 年的好奇号火星车继续开展科学任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479705' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 271</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479705' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48479705'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jet Propulsion Laboratory keeps Curiosity scientifically productive more than a decade after its landing by a combination of autonomous software updates, health monitoring, and power-aware operations. The story illustrates how ground teams sustain long-running robotic missions amid aging hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JPL 通过自动化软件更新、健康监控与电源感知运行等手段，使好奇号在着陆十余年后仍能开展科学研究，展示团队如何在老化硬件条件下维持长期机器任务的持续性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=66880331&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C312%2C0%2C313' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=66880331&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C312%2C0%2C313' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48516504' data-tags='Open Source,Startups,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48516504' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tensorzero/tensorzero' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI OSS tool repo goes archived over night after raising $7.3M Seed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 开源工具仓库融资后一夜之间归档</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 269</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48516504'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI OSS project’s repository was archived overnight after a $7.3M seed round. This highlights how funding does not guarantee long-term maintenance or governance in open-source projects and raises questions about sustainability and stewardship. It illustrates the fragility of OSS ecosystems when strategic priorities shift.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在完成730万美元种子轮融资后，一款 AI 开源工具的代码仓库在一夜之间被归档。这反映了资金并不能确保长期维护与治理，并引发对开源生态系统在战略优先级变化时的可持续性与托管的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/829640443/56e9b8da-eabc-4dff-bac7-73d459e7880d' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/829640443/56e9b8da-eabc-4dff-bac7-73d459e7880d' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lr0.org/blog/p/arabic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The experience of rendering Arabic typography and its technical debt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>渲染阿拉伯语排版的体验与技术债务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48516710'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines the challenges of rendering Arabic typography, including shaping, ligatures, and bidirectional text, and argues that technical debt from these aspects affects UI quality and future-proofing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析阿拉伯语排版在字形、连字和双向文本处理上的挑战，指出相关的技术债务会影响界面质量和未来兼容性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48515454' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://imil.net/blog/posts/2026/rtx-5080-+-rtx-3090-setup-80+-tok-s-on-qwen-3.6-27b-q8/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RTX 5080 and RTX 3090 Setup: 80 Tok/s on Qwen 3.6 27B Q8</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RTX 5080 与 RTX 3090 搭建：在 Qwen 3.6 27B Q8 上实现 80 token/s</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48515454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48515454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48515454'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hardware setup combining RTX 5080 and RTX 3090 achieves about 80 tokens per second on Qwen 3.6 27B Q8. The post demonstrates practical GPU pairing for efficient LLM inference, highlighting throughput, energy use, and cost considerations for enthusiasts and researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 RTX 5080 与 RTX 3090 搭配使用，在 Qwen 3.6 27B Q8 上实现约 80 token/s 的推理速率。此文展示了用于高效大语言模型推理的实际 GPU 配置，重点讨论吞吐量、能耗及成本等考量，适合爱好者与研究人员参考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440383' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440383' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://teenage.engineering/products/apc-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>APC–2 – A professional record cutter for producing original playback discs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>APC-2：专业级刻录机，制作原始播放唱片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 265</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440383'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>APC-2 is a professional vinyl cutter enabling artists to produce original playback discs, bypassing traditional mastering and pressing for small runs. It promises precise cuts and smoother workflow integration with modern audio tools, signaling a DIY resurgence in physical media. For independent artists, it lowers barriers to producing limited edition discs without large-scale facilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>APC-2 是一台专业级刻录机，使艺术家能够制作原创播放唱片，绕过传统的母带与压片流程以实现小批量生产。它承诺高精度切割和与现代音频工具的无缝对接，标志着实体媒体的DIY复兴。对于独立艺术家而言，这意味着无需大规模厂房也能发行限量版唱片。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.teenage.engineering/_img/6a22f2d3ab13747827da71c7_1024.webp' data-full='https://assets.teenage.engineering/_img/6a22f2d3ab13747827da71c7_1024.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48448106' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/apple-events/event-stream/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple WWDC 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果 WWDC 2026</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48448106' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48448106' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 528</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48448106'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s WWDC 2026 preview signals another developer-focused year with software updates, platform features, and possibly new tooling or privacy enhancements. Expect deeper AI-assisted development tools, performance improvements across iOS, macOS, and watchOS, and more integration between services. The conference will indicate Apple&#x27;s priorities for developers this cycle.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果 WWDC 2026 预览透露出另一个以开发者为中心的一年，涵盖软件更新、平台特性及可能的新工具和隐私增强。预计会有更深的 AI 辅助开发工具、各平台的性能提升，以及服务之间更紧密的整合。大会揭示了本轮对开发者的优先方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/v/apple-events/home/aj/images/meta/apple_events__d0b4ek86reaa_og.png?202606081607' data-full='https://www.apple.com/v/apple-events/home/aj/images/meta/apple_events__d0b4ek86reaa_og.png?202606081607' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48497195' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48497195' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/form/codex-for-oss/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex for open source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向开源的软件 Codex</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48497195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48497195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48497195'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI is launching Codex for Open Source, offering OSS maintainers access to Codex-powered tooling to assist with coding tasks, documentation, and maintenance. The program aims to accelerate open-source development while addressing licensing and attribution considerations in collaborative projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 推出面向开源的软件 Codex，向开源项目维护者提供 Codex 驱动的工具，以帮助代码编写、文档撰写和维护工作。这项计划旨在加速开源开发，同时在协作项目中处理许可和署名等问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/6s4MmsQzgkEslCGeLGJDQS/33a57423294f2b72e3bd712d0446c1a8/Codex_For_Open_Source.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/6s4MmsQzgkEslCGeLGJDQS/33a57423294f2b72e3bd712d0446c1a8/Codex_For_Open_Source.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48522486' data-tags='Open Source,Gaming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48522486' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/ReactOS-Running-Half-Life' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ReactOS (FOSS &quot;Windows&quot;) achieves 3D-accelerated Half-Life on real hardware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ReactOS 在真实硬件上实现 3D 加速的 Half-Life</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48522486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 259</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48522486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48522486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ReactOS achieves 3D-accelerated Half-Life running on real hardware, showcasing progress toward a free, Windows-compatible OS capable of modern gaming performance. The achievement hinges on driver compatibility, GPU APIs, and community contributions to reverse-engineered Windows paths. It signals practical milestones for open-source Windows alternatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ReactOS 在真实硬件上实现了 3D 加速的 Half-Life，展示了其向免费、与 Windows 兼容系统迈进、可进行现代游戏的潜力。成就依赖驱动兼容性、GPU API 支持以及社区对逆向工程 Windows 路径的贡献。标志着开源 Windows 替代方案的一个现实里程碑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=reactos_halflife_1' data-full='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=reactos_halflife_1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48455233' data-tags='Finance,Business,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48455233' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.morningstar.com/stocks/why-we-think-spacex-ipo-is-overvalued?content_id=20768396545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We Think the SpaceX IPO Is Overvalued</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们认为 SpaceX 的上市估值被高估</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48455233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 259</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48455233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48455233'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Morningstar argues SpaceX IPO is overvalued given the company&#x27;s private valuation, funding needs, and opaque economics. The note cautions investors about laddering into a hype-driven offering that lacks comparable peers and clear profitability signals. It highlights the risk of tech unicorns taking public exits at lofty multiples.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Morningstar 认为 SpaceX 的上市估值偏高，原因包括私有估值、融资需求与不透明的商业模式。分析提醒投资者不要被热潮带走，缺乏可比公司与明确盈利信号。此举也反映了科技独角兽以高倍数上市的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.morningstar.com/resizer/v2/6E4G26F3ENEKFPETMZ3CXR4L24.jpg?auth=493e6a8ed33abf3fe87c42f5524da8a88b9ca68f6d376dd8e0bc2a4892c431f4&amp;quality=80&amp;height=1350&amp;width=2400&amp;crop=0,0:2400,1350' data-full='https://media.morningstar.com/resizer/v2/6E4G26F3ENEKFPETMZ3CXR4L24.jpg?auth=493e6a8ed33abf3fe87c42f5524da8a88b9ca68f6d376dd8e0bc2a4892c431f4&amp;quality=80&amp;height=1350&amp;width=2400&amp;crop=0,0:2400,1350' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48504063' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48504063' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bytecodealliance.org/articles/WASI-0.3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WASI 0.3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WASI 0.3</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48504063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48504063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48504063'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WASI 0.3 updates broaden the WebAssembly System Interface with new capabilities and stability improvements, enabling more practical system-level access from Wasm runtimes. The change helps developers build portable, sandboxed applications with better I/O, file systems, and networking, while enhancing security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WASI 0.3 提供了更多系统级能力与稳定性改进，使 Wasm 运行时能够更好地访问文件系统、网络和 I/O 等底层资源，同时提升安全性，推动可移植的沙箱应用开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bytecodealliance.org/images/avatar.png' data-full='https://bytecodealliance.org/images/avatar.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48448313' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48448313' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48448313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Why hasn&#x27;t there been a real competitor to Ticketmaster yet?</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/culture/2026/jan/05/raiders-of-the-lost-ark-hit-video-game-prince-of-persia' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How we made hit video game Prince of Persia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们是如何做出《Prince of Persia》这款热卖游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48468852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48468852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48468852'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Guardian piece recounts the development of Prince of Persia, highlighting pioneering animation techniques like rotoscoping and iterative design under hardware constraints. It also touches on the game&#x27;s enduring influence on action-adventure design and cinematic storytelling in games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章回顾《波斯王子》的开发过程，揭示了如光绘运动捕捉等开创性动画技法，以及在硬件限制下的迭代设计。并探讨这款游戏对动作冒险与游戏叙事风格的长期影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/1a9c6d9a50110f8da1e20121b0b5aaabd88266ad/120_0_3092_2474/master/3092.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=e84110ce298a51956080bef453560d4a' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/1a9c6d9a50110f8da1e20121b0b5aaabd88266ad/120_0_3092_2474/master/3092.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=e84110ce298a51956080bef453560d4a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jivx.com/eki' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All 9,300 Japanese train station, animated by the year it opened (1872–2026)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>所有 9,300 个日本车站按开通年份（1872–2026）动画展示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48475100'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data-visualization project animates every Japanese train station by its opening year (1872–2026), revealing how rail networks expanded over more than a century. It offers a striking lens on urban growth and transportation history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个数据可视化项目把日本所有车站按开通年份（1872–2026）逐年动画化，揭示铁路网的扩张与城市化的历史轨迹。它为理解交通与区域发展提供了直观视角。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48484496' data-tags='Legal,Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48484496' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/congress-just-rushed-through-disastrous-copyright-office-overhaul' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>H.R. 6028 would fundamentally change the U.S. Copyright Office</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>H.R. 6028 将从根本改变美国版权局</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48484496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48484496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48484496'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Legislation H.R. 6028 proposes a sweeping overhaul of the US Copyright Office, with implications for IP policy, governance, and oversight, potentially reshaping how fair use and public-domain works are treated. The move could affect AI developers and researchers who rely on copyrighted material for training, necessitating new compliance frameworks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>H.R. 6028 提议对美国版权局进行全面改革，涉及机构治理和监管框架等重大变化，可能重新定义合理使用与公共领域的界限。这一举措对依赖受版权保护材料进行训练的 AI 开发者和研究者将产生影响，需要建立新的合规机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/copyright-digital.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/copyright-digital.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48502601' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.osull.com/2026/06/12/ryanair-dark-ux-patterns-summer-2026-refresher/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ryanair dark UX patterns summer 2026 refresher</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞安航空夏季 2026 暗黑 UX 模式回顾</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48502601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48502601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48502601'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece surveys Ryanair&#x27;s use of dark UX patterns in its booking flow, including opaque pricing, aggressive upsells, and friction-filled steps. It argues these tactics boost conversions at the cost of user trust and transparency, and notes regulatory scrutiny and evolving best practices in travel UX.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章回顾瑞安航空在预订流程中的暗黑 UX 模式，如隐性定价、强力促销和设计上的摩擦点。此类手法在提高转化的同时侵蚀用户信任与透明度，行业规则与最佳实践也在持续演变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.osull.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/image.png' data-full='https://blog.osull.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48528371' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48528371' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/nex-agi/Nex-N2/issues/4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rio de Janeiro&#x27;s &quot;homegrown&quot; LLM appears to be a merge of an existing model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>里约热内卢的‘本土化’大模型似乎是现有模型的合并</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48528371'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Early analysis suggests the Rio de Janeiro project reuses components from an existing LLM rather than training an original one from scratch. This provenance has implications for licensing, attribution, and how the model behaves in practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>初步分析表明，该项目在现有大模型基础上混合组件，而非完全从头训练。这对许可、署名和实际表现等方面都可能产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/04f1551c5a62546c1dd9c9a478b1462b32444b66fbf27065a4fd61c3bcbeff59/nex-agi/Nex-N2/issues/4' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/04f1551c5a62546c1dd9c9a478b1462b32444b66fbf27065a4fd61c3bcbeff59/nex-agi/Nex-N2/issues/4' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48478469' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.extend.ai/ui' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Extend UI – open-source UI kit for modern document apps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Extend UI——用于现代文档应用的开源 UI 套件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48478469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48478469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48478469'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Extend UI is an open-source UI kit designed for building modern document apps, offering modular components for editing, collaboration, and document-centric UX. It aims to speed frontend development while keeping interfaces cohesive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Extend UI 是一个用于构建现代文档应用的开源 UI 套件，提供编辑、协作和文档驱动的组件模块，旨在加速前端开发并保持界面的一致性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48489917' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48489917' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/huggingface/open-r1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open Reproduction of DeepSeek-R1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek-R1 开放复现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 244</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48489917'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The DeepSeek-R1 project opens an open reproduction workflow, enabling researchers to reproduce results and benchmark performance. This enhances transparency and reproducibility in its domain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek-R1 项目公开了复现实验流程，方便研究者重现实验结果并进行性能基准比较。这提升了相关领域的透明度和可重复性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48500404' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48500404' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.korte.co/2026/06/11/digital-sovereignty-becomes-an-imparative-as-the-us-reads-dutch-emails/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Digital Sovereignty Becomes an Imperative as the US Reads Dutch Emails</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数字主权成为必需：美国读取荷兰人的邮件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48500404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 239</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48500404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48500404'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that cross-border data access and surveillance pressures make digital sovereignty an urgent priority for privacy and governance. It calls for policy and architectural measures that localize control, strengthen encryption, and clarify jurisdiction for data handling across borders.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>跨境数据访问与监控压力使数字主权成为隐私与治理的当务之急。文中呼吁在政策与技术层面加强区域化控制、提升数据本地化与加密保护，并明确跨境数据处理的司法管辖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.korte.co/wp-content/uploads/2023/10/pexels-tima-miroshnichenko-6266684-1024x683.jpg' data-full='https://www.korte.co/wp-content/uploads/2023/10/pexels-tima-miroshnichenko-6266684-1024x683.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48508726' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48508726' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.swift.org/blog/migrating-truetype-hinting-to-swift/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Swift at Apple: Migrating the TrueType hinting interpreter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果将 TrueType 提示解释器迁移到 Swift</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48508726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48508726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48508726'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s Swift team is migrating the TrueType hinting interpreter to Swift, part of modernizing font rendering infrastructure. The change aims to improve safety and maintainability, with potential downstream impact on font rendering performance and future optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果的 Swift 团队正在把 TrueType 提示解释器迁移到 Swift，作为现代化字体渲染基础设施的一部分。此变更提升了安全性与可维护性，可能影响现有字体的渲染性能并为未来的优化铺路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://swift.org/apple-touch-icon-180x180.png' data-full='https://swift.org/apple-touch-icon-180x180.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在 M1 Max 上使用本地机器学习对 669 GB 的 GoPro 视频进行离线索引，展示私有化、离线处理的可行性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48475871' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Are most corporate SWE jobs performative?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：大多数企业 SWE 岗位是表演性的吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 233</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 274</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48475871'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Hacker News thread questions whether most corporate software engineering roles are performative, citing bureaucratic incentives, meetings, and metrics that may not translate to real product impact. The discussion spotlights productivity traps and the need for better metrics and streamlined work. It prompts readers to rethink how engineering value is measured in corporate settings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 Hacker News 讨论提出，企业级软件工程师岗位是否更多地受制于官僚激励、会议与表面指标，而非真正的产品影响。讨论揭示生产力陷阱以及需要更有效的衡量标准和精简工作流程的问题，促使人们重新思考企业环境中的工程价值衡量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>学校儿童的业余阅读显著下降（报告显示）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 232</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 305</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48479314'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report shows kids read for pleasure less; implications for literacy, motivation, and lifelong learning. Educators may need targeted interventions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报告显示学生的休闲阅读明显下降，可能影响识字、积极性与终身学习动机。教育者或需采取针对性干预。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>职位空缺：Stonehenge 主管</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48455928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48455928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48455928'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>English Heritage is recruiting a Head of Stonehenge, overseeing conservation, archaeology coordination, and visitor programs for the iconic site. The role signals ongoing investment in heritage and the need for experienced leadership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>English Heritage 正在招聘 Stonehenge 主管，负责遗址保护、考古协调和游客体验等工作。此任命反映了对国家遗产的持续投入以及对资深管理者的需求。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48524620' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48524620' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://garrit.xyz/posts/2026-05-06-dont-trust-large-context-windows' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t trust large context windows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别信任大型上下文窗口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48524620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48524620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48524620'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Large context windows are not a silver bullet for accuracy; models can still hallucinate or be misled by irrelevant or noisy signals. The post advocates architectures that rely on retrieval, modular reasoning, and careful evaluation of what the model actually uses from context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>超大上下文窗口并不能保证推理的可靠性；模型仍可能出现幻觉或被无关信息干扰。文中提倡结合检索、模块化推理和对上下文用量的严格评估来提升系统稳健性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://garrit.xyz/assets/five-years-of-blogging-cover-3d.png' data-full='https://garrit.xyz/assets/five-years-of-blogging-cover-3d.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48470152' data-tags='Education,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48470152' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/computer-science-degree-isnt-dead' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The computer science degree isn’t dead</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>计算机科学学位没死</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48470152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48470152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48470152'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece defends the value of formal CS education in the AI era, arguing that core concepts, rigorous problem solving, and research incentives remain essential. It notes that universities are adapting with AI-enabled tools and new curricula, preserving a strong path into software, systems, and theory. For practitioners, the degree still signals foundational competencies that bootcamps can&#x27;t fully replace.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文为计算机科学学位在人工智能时代的价值辩护，认为核心概念、解决问题的能力和研究导向仍然关键。指出大学正通过与 AI 工具结合的新课程与教学方式进行调整，为软件、系统与理论提供坚实的培养路径。对从业者而言，学位仍然是一种正式的能力信号， Bootcamp 等路径难以完全取代。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.webp?id=65257424&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C150%2C0%2C150' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.webp?id=65257424&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C150%2C0%2C150' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48409474' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48409474' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unix.stackexchange.com/questions/18154/what-is-the-purpose-of-the-lostfound-folder-in-linux-and-unix' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is the purpose of the lost+found folder in Linux and Unix? (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux/Unix 的 lost+found 文件夹有什么用？（2014）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48409474'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The lost+found folder stores inodes recovered after filesystem checks, acting as a repository for orphaned fragments following a crash. It’s mostly of interest to those doing recovery or forensics, not day-to-day users. The feature embodies how Linux/Unix handles recovery while preserving data remnants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>lost+found 文件夹是在 Linux/Unix 文件系统自检修复（fsck）时用于存放恢复的 inode 的位置。它保存着在崩溃后被重建出的碎片和孤儿数据，通常并不面向普通用户。其存在的意义在于协助从损坏的文件系统中找回数据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unix.stackexchange.com/Content/Sites/unix/Img/apple-touch-icon@2.png?v=32fb07f7ce26' data-full='https://unix.stackexchange.com/Content/Sites/unix/Img/apple-touch-icon@2.png?v=32fb07f7ce26' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48444871' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zig by Example：通过示例学习 Zig 语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48444871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48444871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48444871'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zig by Example provides a hands-on guide to the Zig programming language, teaching syntax and idioms through practical samples. It helps readers grasp low-level concepts common in systems programming and compare Zig&#x27;s approach to C and Rust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zig by Example 提供一份以示例驱动的Zig语言学习指南，通过实际代码样例教授语法与常用模式。帮助读者掌握系统编程中的底层概念，并对照C与Rust等语言的设计取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48504912' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://envs.net/~volpe/blog/posts/reduce-slop.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Slightly reducing the sloppiness of AI generated front end</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>稍微降低 AI 生成前端的随意性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48504912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48504912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48504912'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical note on trimming the &#x27;slop&#x27; in AI-generated front ends, offering concrete steps to tighten output: more deterministic styling, stricter prompts, and post-processing checks. The aim is to keep the speed and creativity of AI while delivering reliable, production-ready UI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 生成前端的随意性得到了一些改进建议，提出通过更确定的样式、严格的提示和后处理检查等办法来减少输出的混乱。目标是在保持 AI 快速迭代的同时，交付更稳定、可投入生产的 UI 界面。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retro-hardware project detailing WorkBoy, a hardware addon that turn a Game Boy into a pocket computer with keyboard input and expanded capabilities. It showcases hobbyist ingenuity in repurposing classic hardware for modern usability.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>展望 PostgreSQL 19：查询提示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 212</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48413655'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL 19 is expected to bring query hints, giving developers a way to influence the planner without changing queries. This could improve performance tuning and explain plans for complex workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>预计 PostgreSQL 19 将引入查询提示，使开发者在不修改查询的情况下影响优化器的决策。此举可能提升复杂工作负载的调优效果与执行计划的理解。</p>
