<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48491048' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.tacoda.dev/lisps-influence-on-ruby-6a54f1a7740e' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lisp&#x27;s Influence on Ruby</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lisp 对 Ruby 的影响</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48491048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48491048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48491048'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how Lisp-inspired ideas shaped Ruby&#x27;s flexibility, metaprogramming culture, and a penchant for DSLs, showing that Lisp’s legacy lives on in modern dynamic languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解析 Lisp 传统中的理念如何影响 Ruby 的灵活性、元编程文化以及对领域特定语言的偏好，展示 Lisp 的遗产在现代动态语言中的延续。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://miro.medium.com/v2/da:true/bc1f8416df0cad099e43cda2872716e5864f18a73bda2a7547ea082aca9b5632' data-full='https://miro.medium.com/v2/da:true/bc1f8416df0cad099e43cda2872716e5864f18a73bda2a7547ea082aca9b5632' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48470581' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48470581' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/RichardSSutton/status/2061216087744946656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rich Sutton on AI creativity and discovery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rich Sutton 谈AI的创造力与发现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48470581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48470581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48470581'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rich Sutton argues that AI creativity stems from how we define objectives and exploration, framing discovery as goal-directed search rather than sudden inspiration. The stance helps temper hype by emphasizing evaluation, robustness, and known limits of current AI systems. It invites a pragmatic view of what AI can and cannot do.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rich Sutton 指出，AI 的创造力来自于目标设定与探索的方式，所谓发现其实是面向目标的搜索而非灵光一现。此观点有助于缓解对 AI 突破性的过度炒作，强调评估、鲁棒性以及当前系统的局限性，促使人们以务实的眼光看待 AI 的能力边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1797759934811254786/XsJes4Sk_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1797759934811254786/XsJes4Sk_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48495575' data-tags='AI,Gaming,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48495575' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kennethpayne.uk/p/shall-we-play-a-game' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shall we play a game? My AI nuclear simulation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>要不要来玩个游戏？我的 AI 核武仿真</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48495575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48495575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48495575'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI-driven nuclear simulation is presented as a game-like exploration of escalation scenarios. It raises questions about how powerful AI systems model high-stakes decision making and the ethical boundaries of simulating real-world weapons. The piece invites reflection on safety, control, and the line between playful experimentation and policy risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以游戏化方式展示 AI 对核武升级场景的仿真，引发对强大 AI 如何建模高风险决策的思考以及对在真实武器中的伦理边界的质疑。文中促请读者反思安全、控制与在娱乐性实验与政策风险之间的界线。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!3TAh!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F24302f16-6ee6-4b02-b46e-bfeb0d56efbf_1024x608.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!3TAh!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F24302f16-6ee6-4b02-b46e-bfeb0d56efbf_1024x608.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48528729' data-tags='Security,Open Source,Hardware'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 100 kW FM radio signal in the Ohio Valley was severed in broad daylight, highlighting vulnerabilities in broadcast infrastructure and the potential for sabotage or weather-related outages.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/sloppenheimer-amazon-employees-mock-the-companys-ai-on-slack/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Sloppenheimer:&#x27; Amazon employees mock the company&#x27;s AI on Slack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sloppenheimer：亚马逊员工在 Slack 上嘲笑公司的 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>&quot;Sloppenheimer&quot;： Amazon employees mock the company&#x27;s AI on Slack. Slack threads reveal Amazon employees joking about the company&#x27;s AI, highlighting skepticism about its reliability and alignment with real-world use. The incident underscores how internal sentiment around AI initiatives can influence public perception and strategy. It also reflects broader tensions between ambitious AI deployments and day-to-day product quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Slack 聊天记录显示亚马逊员工在讨论公司 AI 时带着嘲讽与怀疑，质疑其可靠性与应用价值。事件揭示了内部情绪如何影响外部对 AI 计划的看法和实施策略。也反映了在大规模 AI 部署中的野心与日常产品质量之间的张力。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48502321'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Outlines trends for email evolution: privacy-preserving protocols, better interoperability, offline-first clients, and smarter automation. It discusses how federated infrastructure and anti-spam innovations might shape next-gen email experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展望电子邮件的演进方向：隐私保护协议、互操作性提升、离线优先的客户端，以及更智能的自动化。探讨分布式基础设施与反垃圾邮件技术如何塑造下一代邮件体验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48483387' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/innovation/man-created-written-language-cherokee-did-efficiently-elegantly-peers-thought-magic-180988850/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sequoyah’s syllabary created a written language for the Cherokee</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sequoyah 的音节字母为切罗基创造了书写语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48483387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48483387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48483387'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sequoyah&#x27;s Cherokee syllabary enabled a lasting literate culture among the Cherokee, accelerating governance, education, and record-keeping. The invention shows how a writing system can catalyze political resilience and cultural preservation under pressure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sequoyah 的切罗基音节字母让切罗基人建立了持续的书写传统，推动治理、教育与档案管理。这个发明展示了书写体系如何在压力下推动政治韧性与文化传承。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/TMNdwAbB8F7RoCB3h5Xt5VWI8d8=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(612x650:613x651)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/25/77/2577ef6b-781c-4718-822a-9c672456ff5c/sequoyah_smithsonian_concept_b.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/TMNdwAbB8F7RoCB3h5Xt5VWI8d8=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(612x650:613x651)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/25/77/2577ef6b-781c-4718-822a-9c672456ff5c/sequoyah_smithsonian_concept_b.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48476474' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jamanetwork.com/journals/jamanetworkopen/fullarticle/2849122' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vitamin D3 During Pregnancy and Cognitive Performance at 10 Years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>孕期维生素D3与儿童10岁认知表现的研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48435584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 192</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48435584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48435584'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study examines whether Vitamin D3 supplementation during pregnancy correlates with children&#x27;s cognitive performance at age 10, contributing evidence to prenatal nutrition debates. While not proving causation, findings may inform guidelines on vitamin D intake during pregnancy and potential long-term neurodevelopmental effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究评估孕期维生素D3补充是否与儿童10岁时的认知表现相关，为产前营养讨论提供证据。结果并非因果证明，但可能为孕期维生素D补充指南及潜在的长期神经发育影响提供参考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MapComplete 汇聚了可由社区贡献的主题地图，推动开放数据可视化的协作。它降低了跨学科、跨领域的地图创建门槛，让众包信息转化为可视化洞见。此举体现了社群驱动的知识地图的日益兴起。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://garrit.xyz/posts/2026-06-13-pac-man-but-you-re-the-ghost' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pac-Man, but you&#x27;re the ghost</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>吃豆人：你来扮演鬼魂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48524135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48524135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48524135'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reimagines Pac-Man by letting players control the pursuing ghost, flipping the usual chase dynamics. It highlights how changing the player&#x27;s role can illuminate core tropes of classic arcade design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>把吃豆人游戏反向设定成玩家扮演追逐者的幽灵，颠覆了传统的追逐规则。介绍了新的机制、关卡设计，以及这一改动对策略和跑分潜力的影响。通过改变玩家角色来揭示经典街机设计的核心要素。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://areweguiyet.com/#ecosystem' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The state of building user interfaces in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 构建用户界面的现状</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48479008'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Rust UI scene is growing but still lacks a single dominant framework. Projects like egui, Dioxus, Iced, and GTK bindings push forward, yet tradeoffs around portability, ergonomics, and performance persist. The landscape matters for developers evaluating Rust for front-end or tool UIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rust 构建用户界面的生态正在发展，但尚未出现一个主导的框架。诸如 egui、Dioxus、Iced 与 GTK 绑定等项目持续推进，但在可移植性、易用性与性能之间仍存在权衡。对于评估在前端或工具 UI 中使用 Rust 的开发者来说，这一生态格局很关键。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Phoenix LiveView 1.2 continues the trend of building rich, real-time UIs with minimal client-side JS by focusing on stability and API enhancements. <p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.matteast.io/spacex-escape-velocity.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why SpaceX 2040 Revenue FCST $4.3T in highly unlikely</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX 2040 年收入预测达 4.3 万亿美元几乎不可能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48479947'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article deconstructs SpaceX&#x27;s optimistic 2040 revenue forecast of $4.3 trillion, questioning assumptions about launch demand, Starlink monetization, and regulatory timelines. It argues that such rosy projections are unlikely and advises caution for investors modeling SpaceX’s growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对 SpaceX 到 2040 年4.3万亿美元的收入预测进行了拆解，质疑对发射需求、星链盈利与监管时间表等假设的乐观性。结论是此类乐观预测极不可能实现，提醒投资者在估值模型中采用更保守的前提。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yalereview.org/article/sheila-liming-the-end-of-books' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A dumpster arrived behind my university&#x27;s library</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>校园图书馆后面的垃圾箱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48504543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48504543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48504543'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A dumpster behind a university library becomes a lens on how digital access is changing libraries and the fate of physical books. The piece questions whether mass digitization and disposal practices threaten the cultural value and longevity of printed works.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一辆垃圾箱落在校园图书馆后院，成为探讨数字化如何改变图书馆及纸质书命运的隐喻。文章质疑大规模数字化与处置是否会削弱纸质书的文化价值与长期保存。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48475938' data-tags='Design,Hardware'>

<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danq.me/2026/06/09/fn-keys/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Hate (Most) Keyboard &#x27;Fn&#x27; Keys</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我讨厌（大多数）键盘 Fn 键</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48475938'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fn keys are often poorly designed and inconsistent across devices, complicating daily productivity. The piece calls for easier customization, better defaults, and more intuitive keyboard ergonomics to improve the user experience. It argues for rethinking how manufacturers implement Fn keys.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fn 键在大量设备上设计常常不理想且缺乏一致性，影响日常生产力。文章倡导更易自定义、默认设置更直观，以及改善键盘的人机工程学，以提升使用体验，并呼吁厂商重新考虑 Fn 键的实现方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newscientist.com/article/2529849-fully-autonomous-drones-have-killed-human-soldiers-for-the-first-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fully autonomous drones have killed human soldiers for the first time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全自动无人机首次击杀人类士兵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48476214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48476214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48476214'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fully autonomous drones have killed human soldiers for the first time, raising urgent ethical, legal, and strategic questions about the deployment of fully autonomous weapons.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>完全自主无人机首次致使士兵死亡，引发关于自治武器部署的伦理、法律与战略层面的紧迫讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48446909' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48446909' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-01780-6' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are so many young people getting cancer?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么如此多的年轻人患癌？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48446909'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nature reports rising cancer rates among young people, prompting questions about environmental, lifestyle, and genetic factors. The article emphasizes the need for more research into causes, early detection, and equitable access to care. It underscores that the trend has broad implications for health tech and policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nature 报道显示年轻人中的癌症发病率在上升，引发关于环境、生活方式与遗传因素的讨论。文章强调需要更多研究来揭示病因、推进早期检测并确保公平的治疗机会。这一趋势对健康科技与公共政策具有广泛影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-01780-6/d41586-026-01780-6_52505540.jpg' data-full='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-01780-6/d41586-026-01780-6_52505540.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://new.roman-names.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I am building a map of people who lived in the Roman Empire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：我在建立一张居住在罗马帝国时期的人的地图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48481400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48481400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48481400'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hobbyist project maps historical figures to places in the Roman Empire, using historical records to visualize ancient demographics. The effort demonstrates crowdsourcing, data visualization, and ongoing data curation with potential value for historians and educators, while raising questions about source quality and representation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个爱好者项目尝试把历史人物与罗马帝国时期的居住地点对应起来，通过历史记录可视化古代人口分布。该工作展示了众包、数据可视化与持续的数据整理在历史教育与研究中的潜在价值，同时也引发对数据源质量与表示方式的讨论。</p>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A federal judge blocked a proposed $100,000 H1B visa filing fee, delaying a policy change that would affect tech hiring. The decision highlights tensions between immigration policy reform and workforce needs in tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联邦法官阻止了一项关于 H1B 签证申请费 10 万美元的提议，阻滞了可能影响科技招聘的政策变动。这一裁决凸显了移民政策改革与科技行业人力需求之间的博弈。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://doug.sh/posts/erm-a-local-cli-that-strips-ums-uhs-and-erms-from-speech/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Removing &#x27;um&#x27; from a recording is harder than it sounds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将“嗯”等口头语从录音中删除比想象的要难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48498421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48498421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48498421'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A local CLI tool offers to strip filler words like um/uh/er from speech, but achieving natural-sounding removal without distorting cadence is surprisingly hard. The post demonstrates the nuanced trade-offs between intelligibility, timing, and editor-like automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个本地命令行工具尝试从语音中去除口头填充词如“嗯/啊/呃”，但在不破坏语速与自然感的情况下实现完全去除却相当困难。文中展示了可理解性、节奏感与编辑式自动化之间的细微取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues for proactive governance as AI capabilities accelerate, stressing safety, alignment, and risk management at scale. It outlines practical policy ideas to guide responsible development without stifling innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张在 AI 能力快速提升的背景下进行前瞻性治理，强调安全、对齐与大规模风险管理。提出在不扼杀创新的前提下引导负责任发展的具体政策思路。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48487897' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vale.rocks/posts/game-console-browsers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Web Browsers on Video Game Consoles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电子游戏主机上的网页浏览器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48487897' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48487897' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48487897'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An overview of running web browsers on game consoles, highlighting constraints such as sandboxing, memory limits, and performance trade-offs. It discusses how browser experiences on consoles differ from desktop and mobile, and why this matters for web apps and standards on constrained devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概述在游戏主机上运行网页浏览器的情况，强调沙箱、安全、内存和性能等约束。比较游戏主机上的浏览体验与桌面/移动端的差异，解释这对在受限设备上实现网页应用和网络标准的重要性。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48443258' data-tags='Career,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48443258' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://human-in-the-loop.bearblog.dev/replies-to-comments-on-my-llms-are-eroding-my-career-post/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Replies to comments on my &quot;LLMs are eroding my career&quot; post</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对我《LLMs 正在侵蚀我的职业生涯》一文的评论回复</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48443258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48443258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48443258'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Replies add nuance to a heated debate: some commentators warn that LLMs will subsume routine tasks, while others argue they can augment professionals when used as intelligent tools. The thread emphasizes upskilling, specialization, and designing workflows that combine human judgment with AI capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回复为激烈辩论增添了细腻观点：部分评论者警告大模型将取代常规任务，而另一些则认为若作为智能工具使用，能提高专业人员的生产力。讨论强调提升技能、专业化，以及设计能将人类判断与 AI 能力结合的工作流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/human-in-the-loop/pawel-czerwinski-mv3apn9e7su-unsplash-1.webp' data-full='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/human-in-the-loop/pawel-czerwinski-mv3apn9e7su-unsplash-1.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sgaud.com/texts/pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Adaptive PDFs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自适应概率密度函数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48506209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48506209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48506209'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical note on adaptive PDFs discusses how to adjust probability distributions on the fly to improve sampling efficiency and estimation accuracy. It covers algorithms and potential applications in simulations, rendering, and probabilistic modeling, highlighting practical benefits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇技术笔记介绍自适应概率密度函数，讲述如何在运行时动态调整分布以提升采样效率和估计精度。内容涵盖相关算法及在仿真、渲染和概率建模等领域的潜在应用，强调实际收益。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48442569' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48442569' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ms365news.com/blogs/f/your-onedrive-data-now-has-an-expiry-data' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OneDrive data now has an expiry date</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OneDrive 数据现在有到期日期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48442569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48442569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48442569'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft is rolling out an expiry mechanism for OneDrive data, enabling automatic deletion or renewal rules for files and shared items after a defined period. The change reshapes data lifecycle management, retention policies, and user expectations around what persists in the cloud.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软开始在 OneDrive 中引入到期机制，允许对文件和共享项设定过期规则，逾期后自动删除或触发续存。此举影响数据生命周期管理、合规策略以及用户对云端数据可持续性的预期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img1.wsimg.com/isteam/ip/15fca514-39b4-488f-84fb-c340a5dc0f6e/Designer-be54e3b.jpeg' data-full='https://img1.wsimg.com/isteam/ip/15fca514-39b4-488f-84fb-c340a5dc0f6e/Designer-be54e3b.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨形式化方法在编程实践、验证和软件工程未来中的作用，权衡严格性与实际可行性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.aheymans.xyz/post/thinkpad_x61/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Porting the ThinkPad X61 to Coreboot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ThinkPad X61 的 Coreboot 移植</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48456245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48456245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48456245'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Porting ThinkPad X61 to Coreboot demonstrates reviving an aging laptop with open firmware, delivering faster boot times and a transparent, auditable BIOS. The project highlights the appeal of open-source firmware for security-conscious users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ThinkPad X61 的 Coreboot 移植展示了用开源固件重活这台老笔记本，提升开机速度并获得可审计的 BIOS。该项目凸显了开放固件在安全性和可控性方面的吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.aheymans.xyz/thinkpad-addication.jpg' data-full='https://blog.aheymans.xyz/thinkpad-addication.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48477851' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/fcj3088jg1wx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Authentication issues related to API requests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>与 API 请求相关的认证问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48477851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48477851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48477851'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports authentication failures affecting API clients, with 401s and token issues causing disrupted workflows. The note explains ongoing fixes and urges developers to refresh credentials and retry after updates, highlighting the fragility of token-based systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>记录 API 客户端遭遇的认证失败，导致工作流中断及 401 错误。更新指引提及正在修复并建议开发者在更新后刷新凭证并重试，凸显基于令牌的认证系统的脆弱性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-twitter_logos/original/36420/GitHub-Mark-120px-plus.png' data-full='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-twitter_logos/original/36420/GitHub-Mark-120px-plus.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440008' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/melastmohican/rust-rpico2-embassy-examples' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Matter Wi-Fi Light Bulb in Rust on the Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Matter Wi-Fi 灯泡：在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上用 Rust 实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440008'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates building a Matter-enabled Wi‑Fi light bulb using Rust on the Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showcasing a secure, memory-safe IoT path. It highlights how Rust and open standards can simplify building reliable smart home devices on tiny microcontrollers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上用 Rust 构建符合 Matter 标准的 Wi‑Fi 灯泡，体现了一个安全、内存安全的物联网实现路径。该项目展示了 Rust 与开源标准如何简化在微控制器上开发可靠的智能家居设备。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48493564' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48493564' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.righto.com/2012/02/apple-didnt-revolutionize-power.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple didn&#x27;t revolutionize power supplies; new transistors did (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果没有彻底改变电源供应；新晶体管才是关键</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48493564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48493564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48493564'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the real driver behind modern efficiency gains isn’t design of power supplies per se, but advances in transistor technology. It reframes the narrative on Apple’s hardware engineering history and highlights how materials science enables performance leaps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为现代效率提升的真正驱动并非电源设计本身，而是晶体管技术的进步。它重新审视了苹果硬件工程的叙事，强调材料科学如何驱动性能跃升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/blogger_img_proxy/AEn0k_tMbX4tIakCRB4nSKvrQeXmBKSslfsOXU4pXULvWpce6moH7EpbmCK3utR1lTEJozdaWtD3DAWS96g23B_C8VNtGzdwKZA6sc_Y8S3CL9hPvNA=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/blogger_img_proxy/AEn0k_tMbX4tIakCRB4nSKvrQeXmBKSslfsOXU4pXULvWpce6moH7EpbmCK3utR1lTEJozdaWtD3DAWS96g23B_C8VNtGzdwKZA6sc_Y8S3CL9hPvNA=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48467437' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48467437' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brentfitzgerald.com/posts/appreciating-exif/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Appreciating Exif</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欣赏 Exif 数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48467437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48467437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48467437'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Exif metadata often goes overlooked despite its practical value for organizing, debugging, and tracing image provenance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Exif 元数据在组织、调试和追踪图片来源方面极具实用价值，但常被忽视。本文强调元数据如何丰富照片信息，以及随之而来的隐私考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://brentfitzgerald.com/posts/appreciating-exif/index.png' data-full='https://brentfitzgerald.com/posts/appreciating-exif/index.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48402137' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48402137' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ocamlpro.com/blog/2025_07_29_ocaml_onboarding_introduction_to_dune/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OCaml Onboarding: Introduction to the Dune build system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OCaml 入门：Dune 构建系统导览</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48402137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48402137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48402137'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dune is the de facto build system for OCaml projects, handling dependencies, builds, and packaging with sensible defaults. This onboarding guide helps newcomers set up projects quickly and learn best practices for reproducible builds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dune 是 OCaml 的事实标准构建系统，负责依赖、构建和打包。这份入门指南帮助新手快速搭建项目，并学习可重复构建的最佳实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ocamlpro.com/blog/assets/img/ai_construction_camel.png' data-full='https://ocamlpro.com/blog/assets/img/ai_construction_camel.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48450065' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48450065' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://burrito.bio/essays/what-limits-a-cells-size' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are cells small?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>细胞为什么要小</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48450065'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece explains physical and energetic limits that keep cells small, focusing on surface-to-volume ratio and nutrient diffusion. It links these constraints to cellular organization and efficiency trade-offs in biology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解释限制细胞大小的物理与能量约束，聚焦表面积与体积比对养分交换的影响以及扩散过程。将这些约束放在细胞组织与代谢效率的更大框架中，说明生物在效率与复杂性之间的取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48462759' data-tags='Programming,Web,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48462759' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.pyodide.org/posts/314-release/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pyodide 314.0: Python packages can now publish WebAssembly wheels to PyPI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pyodide 314.0：Python 包现在可将 WebAssembly wheel 发布到 PyPI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48462759'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pyodide 314.0 enables Python packages to publish WebAssembly wheels directly to PyPI, lowering the barrier for running Python in the browser and on edge environments. By standardizing WASM wheels, developers can distribute browser-ready Python code alongside native wheels, accelerating web-based Python apps and computational notebooks. Expect changes to packaging workflows, compatibility considerations, and potential security tradeoffs when shipping browser-optimized Python.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pyodide 314.0 现已支持将 WebAssembly wheel 直接发布到 PyPI，降低在浏览器和边缘环境中运行 Python 的门槛。标准化 WASM wheel 让开发者能够把浏览器就绪的 Python 代码与原生轮子一同分发，加速网页端 Python 应用和计算笔记本的部署。这一举措也将带来打包流程、兼容性与在浏览器中执行的安全性权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.pyodide.org/pyodide-logo-small.png' data-full='https://blog.pyodide.org/pyodide-logo-small.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magicvinyldigital.net/2025/04/27/vinyl-succumbs-to-loudness-war-more-than-just-collateral-damage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vinyl succumbs to Loudness War: more than just collateral damage (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑胶在响度战争中屈服（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 237</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48427421'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vinyl is feeling the effects of the loudness war, as mastering trends push for louder tracks on vinyl pressings. That pressure erodes dynamic range and archival quality, and the piece frames it as more than cosmetic damage by highlighting long-term listening experience and market dynamics. It also calls for preserving dynamics and exploring mastering approaches tailored for vinyl to minimize collateral damage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文指出黑胶唱片正在受响度战争影响，母带制作趋向更大音量和压缩，削弱动态范围。此现象不仅影响即时聆听，也危及长期存档与真实音色的再现。文章呼吁更重视保留动态范围，并探索适合黑胶的母带策略，以减少对音质的损害。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/magicvinyldigital.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/Vinyl-LoudnessWar-Small.jpg?fit=500%2C500&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/magicvinyldigital.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/Vinyl-LoudnessWar-Small.jpg?fit=500%2C500&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HelixDB is a graph database designed atop object storage, offering scalable, cost-effective graph workloads by decoupling storage from compute. It explores data model, indexing strategies, and performance trade-offs, highlighting a novel architecture for graphs at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HelixDB 是一个建立在对象存储之上的图数据库，通过将存储与计算分离实现可扩展、成本友好的图工作负载。文章探讨数据模型、索引策略与性能权衡，呈现适用于大规模图数据的新架构。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48447111'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Old&#x27;aVista curates a comprehensive guide to the old Internet, collecting vintage sites and artifacts. It offers historical context for how the Web evolved and practical pointers for building retro experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Old&#x27;aVista 汇集了旧互联网的经典站点和工具，为研究早期网页演变提供线索。它既是怀旧指南，也是理解互联网如何发展、并为复古项目提供参考的资源。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is Grep All You Need? How Agent Harnesses Reshape Agentic Search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grep 就是你要的吗？Agent Harness 重塑面向代理的搜索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48460863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48460863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48460863'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discussion of how Agent Harness and similar tool-using agents rely on simple primitives like grep to perform search and tool calls, arguing that such building blocks can redefine agentic search. The piece analyzes architecture trade-offs and implications for reliability and efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论了 Agent Harness 等工具型代理如何依赖像 grep 这样的简单原语来执行搜索和工具调用，认为这些基本构件可以重新定义代理驱动的搜索。文章分析了架构权衡及对可靠性和效率的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kekcleader/FreeOberon' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FreeOberon – Open-Source, Cross-Platform, Free Pascal/Turbo Pascal-Like Language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FreeOberon：开源、跨平台的 Oberon 风格语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48490927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48490927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FreeOberon presents an open-source, cross-platform take on the Oberon language, offering Pascal-like syntax and a compact toolchain. It aims to rekindle interest in language design from the Niklaus Wirth era while staying usable on modern systems. The project is of interest to language designers and hobbyists exploring minimal, robust toolchains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FreeOberon 提供开源、跨平台的 Oberon 语言实现，保留 Pascal 风格的语法与紧凑的工具链。旨在唤起人们对尼古拉斯·沃尔特时代语言设计的兴趣，同时在现代系统上保持可用。对于语言设计者和热衷于极简且稳健工具链的爱好者有一定吸引力。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48431213'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>On the 100th anniversary of The Adventures of Prince Achmed (1926), the world&#x27;s oldest surviving feature-length animated film, the piece highlights Lotte Reiniger&#x27;s pioneering silhouette animation and its enduring influence on cinema.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>庆祝《 Prince Achmed 的冒险》（1926）成为现存最早的长篇动画片一百周年，文章回顾路特·赖尼格尔的剪影动画及其对电影史的深远影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440549' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://algorithmichiring.github.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Algorithmic Monocultures in Hiring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>招聘中的算法单一化问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440549'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project argues that automated hiring pipelines can create algorithmic monocultures by over-filtering for narrow signals. It warns of bias amplification and reduced diversity, and advocates for transparent features, human-in-the-loop checks, and diverse training data. The takeaway is to design hiring tools that broaden rather than narrow candidate pools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目指出自动化招聘流程可能通过过度依赖狭窄信号而形成算法性单一化。它警示偏见放大和多样性下降，并主张采用透明特征、人工干预和多样化训练数据。核心要点是让招聘工具拓宽而非缩窄候选人池。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://algorithmichiring.github.io/figures/fig1_pipeline.png' data-full='https://algorithmichiring.github.io/figures/fig1_pipeline.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48503940'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece assesses sunscreen regulations, arguing European formulations generally avoid certain problematic ingredients used in the U.S. as of 2024. It discusses regulatory differences, consumer safety implications, and practical tips for choosing safer products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章比较欧洲与美国在防晒成分上的监管差异，认为截至2024年欧洲产品通常采用更安全的配方，避免部分美国常用成分。并就如何选购更安全的防晒产品给出建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(239) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48506850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Why is there some sort of a scam website being advertised on HN?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：为何在 Hacker News 上有广告宣传诈骗网站？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48506850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48506850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48506850'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post questions why Hacker News displays ads linking to scam sites, highlighting ad-network vetting gaps and the broader risks of online advertising. It calls for stronger controls and transparency around what gets promoted to readers in a free, ad-supported model.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该帖质疑 Hacker News 为什么会投放指向诈骗网站的广告，揭示了广告网络审核的漏洞与在线广告的普遍风险。讨论呼吁在免费、广告支撑的模式下加强推广内容的透明度与管控。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48458257' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(240) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://omnia.sas.upenn.edu/story/biologist-scott-poethig-plants-never-age' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Forever Young: how one molecule can lock plants in a youthful state (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>永葆青春：一种分子让植物保持年轻状态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48458257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48458257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48458257'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A molecule appears to lock plants in a youthful state, slowing aging in tissues. If scalable, this could boost crop vigor and yields, but practical deployment faces long-term safety, environmental, and regulatory questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示一种分子能够让植物保持年轻状态，延缓组织衰老。若能在农业上落地，或提升作物活力和产量，但长期安全性、环境影响与监管问题将是关键挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48505452' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48505452' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(242) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/where-did-earth-get-its-oceans-maybe-it-made-them-itself-20260612/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Where Did Earth Get Its Oceans? Maybe It Made Them Itself</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>地球的海洋从何而来？也许是地球自己制造的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48505452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48505452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48505452'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quanta Magazine explores a growing idea that Earth’s oceans may have formed in situ through geochemical processes during accretion, rather than arriving primarily via late delivery by comets. If correct, this reframes the timeline of Earth&#x27;s habitability and informs how we search for oceans on other worlds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Quanta Magazine 探讨一种日益受关注的观点，即地球的海洋可能是在地球本身逐渐形成的地球化学过程中产生，而非主要通过彗星等晚期送达。若正确，将重新定义地球可居住性时间线，并影响我们在其他天体上寻找海洋的思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Origins-of-Earth-Water-cr-Ada-Zejun-Shen-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Origins-of-Earth-Water-cr-Ada-Zejun-Shen-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章聚焦于克隆 Sennheiser BA2015 电池组，涉及对电池组单元、保护电路与外形的逆向分析。内容强调在延长硬件寿命时的安全风险与可行替代方案，并体现黑客精神与实用性之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48507986' data-tags='Business,Energy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48507986' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/renault-electric-vehicle-orders-have-surged-since-start-iran-war-says-ceo-2026-06-10/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EV demand up 50% in France and Germany since Iran war</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自伊朗战争以来，法国和德国新能源汽车需求上涨50%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48507986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48507986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48507986'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Renault’s CEO notes EV demand in France and Germany has surged about 50% since the Iran conflict intensified, signaling strong policy and consumer momentum toward electrification in Europe. The spike could tighten EV supply, drive grid and charging infrastructure investments, and reshape the European auto market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>雷诺首席执行官表示，法国和德国的电动汽车需求在伊朗冲突加剧后约上涨50%，反映出欧洲在政策和消费者层面的强劲电气化动能。需求激增可能收紧车型供应、推动充电与电网基础设施投资，并改变欧洲汽车市场格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A user reports Meta&#x27;s services are down, affecting platforms like Facebook, Instagram, and WhatsApp. The outage highlights how a single company&#x27;s downtime can ripple through global social and commerce channels, underscoring the importance of reliability and resilient fallback options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用户报告 Meta 的服务宕机，影响 Facebook、Instagram 和 WhatsApp 等平台。此次故障凸显单一公司停机对全球社交与数字经济的连锁影响，也强调提升系统可靠性和弹性备选方案的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48464333' data-tags='Career,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48464333' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apollo.com/wealth/the-daily-spark/where-is-the-ai-jobs-crisis' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Where is the AI jobs crisis?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 就业危机在哪儿？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48464333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48464333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48464333'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Where is the AI jobs crisis? The piece challenges the idea of a looming shortfall, noting ongoing demand across industries, skill gaps, and the need for retraining and policy support. It suggests that, despite hype, AI roles remain robust and evolving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑是否真存在 AI 就业危机，指出各行业对 AI 技能的需求持续增长，存在技能缺口与再培训需求。总体观点是 AI 职位需求仍在增长，尚未出现普遍性的危机。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apollo.com/content/dam/apolloaem/apollo-default-page-share-image/apollo-og-image.png' data-full='https://www.apollo.com/content/dam/apolloaem/apollo-default-page-share-image/apollo-og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48462156' data-tags='Energy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48462156' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cleantechnica.com/2026/06/08/solar-energy-saves-europeans-135-million-a-day/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solar Energy Saves Europeans $135M a Day</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48462156'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solar energy saves Europeans about $135 million each day by avoiding fuel costs and price volatility. The figure underscores solar&#x27;s role in energy security and economic resilience as Europe expands its solar capacity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>太阳能每天为欧洲带来约1.35亿美元的节省，来自避免燃料成本和电力价格波动。这一数字凸显太阳能在能源安全和经济韧性中的作用，随着欧洲继续扩大光伏装机。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 TI-84 Plus 操作系统进行的全面逆向工程，揭示计算器固件的内部工作原理、文件系统、应用加载与安全边界。这类工作推动复古计算社区，也显示了经典硬件的长期可维护性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CSS 的不可避免的坏部分</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48459678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48459678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48459678'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CSS has endemic &#x27;bad parts&#x27; that designers must learn to work around, from selector specificity to layout quirks. The post argues these design flaws are not mistakes but systemic constraints that require clever patterns and mental models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CSS 存在天生的‘坏部分’，比如选择器的特异性和布局怪癖，往往需要设计者去绕行。文中认为这些缺陷不是错误，而是系统性约束，需要通过巧妙的模式与思维模型来应对。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tratt.net/laurie/blog/2026/test_case_reducers_are_underappreciated_debugging_tools.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Test-case reducers are underappreciated debugging tools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>测试用例缩减工具被低估的调试利器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48459659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48459659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48459659'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Test-case reducers help isolate minimal failing scenarios, dramatically speeding debugging and regression testing. The post surveys practical techniques and real-world examples for trimming test cases without sacrificing reproducibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>测试用例缩减工具有助于将故障场景缩小到最小可复现的情况，从而显著提升调试和回归测试的效率。文中介绍了实用技巧和真实案例，展示在不牺牲可复现性的前提下如何精简测试用例。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c14yz5jg476o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Richard Scolyer Has Died</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48441242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48441242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48441242'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Renowned Australian melanoma pathologist Richard Scolyer has died, leaving a major legacy in cancer genomics and diagnostic pathology. Tributes praise his contributions to melanoma research, international collaborations, and the standardization of diagnostic practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>澳大利亚知名黑素瘤病理学家理查德·斯科卢耶去世，长期致力于癌症基因组学与诊断病理学领域。 各界对他的贡献表示缅怀，肯定他在黑色素瘤研究、国际合作与诊断规范化方面的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉全自动驾驶在丹麦批准视频中使用自行车道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48507248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48507248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a Denmark regulatory video, Tesla&#x27;s Full Self-Driving footage appears to drive in a bicycle lane, with an early error noted around 12 seconds. The clip has sparked scrutiny of demonstration practices for regulatory approvals and raises questions about the gap between marketing demonstrations and real-world safety performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在丹麦的监管视频中，特斯拉全自动驾驶的镜头似乎驶入自行车道，并在约12秒处出现了早期错误。这段视频引发对监管审批演示做法的质疑，并提示营销演示与真实路况下的安全表现之间的差距。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.podman.io/2025/10/podman-6-machine-usability-improvements/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Podman 6: machine usability improvements (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Podman 6：机器可用性改进（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48434963'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Podman 6 delivers machine usability improvements, simplifying how developers interact with machine-based workflows in the container ecosystem. The update tightens defaults, improves CLI ergonomics, and strengthens rootless operations. It lowers the barrier to productive, daemonless container workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Podman 6 对机器相关命令带来可用性改进，简化了上手与生命周期管理，降低非 Root 模式下使用容器的门槛。更新聚焦于提高开发者在日常容器工作流中的效率与自动化能力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://planetscale.com/blog/the-only-scalable-delete' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The only scalable delete in Postgres is DROP TABLE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Postgres 中唯一可扩展的删除操作是 DROP TABLE</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48492822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48492822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48492822'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that deleting vast amounts of data in Postgres scales best with dropping tables rather than row-level DELETEs; discusses implications for archival strategies and schema design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出在 Postgres 中，海量数据的删除最具可扩展性的做法是 DROP TABLE，而非逐行 DELETE；并探讨归档与模式设计的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://planetscale.com/assets/the-only-scalable-delete-social-DZ748lqJ.png' data-full='https://planetscale.com/assets/the-only-scalable-delete-social-DZ748lqJ.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can LLMs Beat Classical Hyperparameter Optimization Algorithms?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型能否超越经典超参数优化算法？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48462062'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers test whether LLMs can outperform classical hyperparameter optimization (HPO) algorithms. Early results show LLMs can propose promising hyperparameter configurations, but reliability and reproducibility remain open questions. If validated, this could influence AutoML workflows by reducing search costs and accelerating experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员评估大型语言模型是否能超越经典超参数优化算法。初步结果显示 LLMs 在给出有前景的超参数配置方面有潜力，但可靠性与可复现性仍待解决。若证实优越性，可能改变 AutoML 的工作流，降低搜索成本、提速实验。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48501012' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(286) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sseh.uchicago.edu/doc/roauss.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Report on an Unidentified Space Station – J.G. Ballard (1982)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>未识别太空站的报告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48501012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48501012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48501012'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The report examines evidence around an unidentified space station and the challenges of confirming it, including tracking, orbital data uncertainties, and observational signatures. It discusses implications for space situational awareness and scientific catalogs, and highlights how such cases test cooperation among agencies and the reliability of early-warning signals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该报告探讨了有关未识别太空站的证据与确认难题，包括跟踪、轨道数据的不确定性以及观测信号。它讨论此类事件对太空态势感知与科学目录的影响，并强调各机构协作与早期预警系统在此类情景中的作用与挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful essay on automating repetitive development tasks, workflows, and decision processes to reduce manual coding. The piece serves as a pragmatic guide for engineers embracing productivity automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者讨论通过自动化来减少开发中的重复性工作与决策步骤。结合工具链与自动化实践，强调人机协作与适度的监督。为拥抱生产力自动化的工程师提供务实经验与教训。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大规模数据中心的扁平化网络（亚马逊）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48456048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48456048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon describes a flat datacenter network design used to scale efficiently, reduce latency, and simplify operations across massive facilities. The architecture emphasizes uniform fabrics and large-scale routing to deliver predictable performance at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊描述了一种扁平化数据中心网络设计，旨在高效扩展、降低延迟并简化大规模设施的运维。该架构强调统一的网络织物与大规模路由，以在大规模环境中提供可预测的性能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/garethgeorge/backrest' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Backrest – a web UI and orchestrator for restic backup</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48370278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48370278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48446532'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Voronoi diagrams partition the world into regions around each capital, illustrating geographic proximity without relying on borders. The visualization makes patterns in global capital placement, highlighting cluster regions and spacing between major hubs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该作品用 Voronoi 图把世界各首都分区展示出来，每个首都成为一个区域的中心点，边界由最近的首都决定。图像揭示全球首都的分布规律和区域聚集趋势，便于理解地理接近度与影响力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48527766' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48482293' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48482293' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sas.upenn.edu/~cavitch/pdf-library/Nagel_Bat.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is it like to be a bat? (1974) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成为一只蝙蝠是什么感觉？（1974）[PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48482293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48482293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48482293'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thomas Nagel argues that knowing a bat&#x27;s physiology doesn&#x27;t reveal what it is like to be a bat, highlighting the hard problem of consciousness. The paper advances the idea that subjective experience resists reduction to physical descriptions, shaping debates in philosophy of mind and AI ethics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>奈格尔在这篇论文中主张，即使能全面了解蝙蝠的生理，也无法知道它的主观感受是什么。这揭示意识的主观性难以被完全还原为客观物理描述，并影响后续的心智哲学与 AI 伦理讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/06/12/arts/design/david-hockney-dead.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>David Hockney, Who Restored the Human Form to Art, Dies at 88</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让人体回归艺术的霍克尼去世，享年88岁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48502730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48502730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48502730'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>David Hockney, a pivotal figure who reframed how the human form is depicted, has died at 88. His experiments with drawing, painting, photography, and digital media reshaped modern art and influenced generations of artists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大卫·霍克尼是以重新塑造人体在艺术中的地位而著称的艺术家，如今去世，享年 88 岁。其在绘画、摄影及数字媒介上的探索深刻影响了现代艺术与后续艺术家的创作观。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48467759'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Exif Smuggling demonstrates how EXIF metadata can be abused to smuggle payloads or bypass filters. It underscores the need for stringent metadata sanitization in image-handling pipelines and vigilant validation of uploaded media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Exif 媒体元数据走私攻击演示了如何利用 EXIF 元数据携带数据或绕过筛选。提醒在图片处理管线中对元数据进行严格清洗和有效性校验。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(305) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.righto.com/2026/06/ibm-604-thyraton-tube-module.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Powering up a module from the IBM 604: an electronic calculator from 1948</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为 IBM 604 的模块上电：1948 年电子计算器的复原之旅</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48436819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48436819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48436819'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on look at powering up a modular unit from the IBM 604, a 1948 vacuum-tube calculator. It covers the challenges of reviving vintage hardware, including high-voltage power supplies and precise on/off sequencing. The project offers a tangible window into the engineering trade-offs that paved the way for later transistorized machines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章实地讲解如何为 IBM 604 的一个模块上电，展示了1948年真空管计算器的复原难点。内容聚焦高压供电、上电顺序等技术挑战，以及这类早期硬件在设计取舍上的历史教训。通过复原，可以直观感受推动后续晶体管化的工程演进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/blogger_img_proxy/AEn0k_s-NkbGPu7trHfdYh_gu7RvAz0_q1BuYfAxj2yqQopSBnd-pumfnVjtLnB6_zE0hNKtvIBmvxm75X9N5vFxL4UZr3dzJPL8Jg9CCyp3Il1F8fK_o3LqTKDRB3mCYadmcaZhoagZ1Hk=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/blogger_img_proxy/AEn0k_s-NkbGPu7trHfdYh_gu7RvAz0_q1BuYfAxj2yqQopSBnd-pumfnVjtLnB6_zE0hNKtvIBmvxm75X9N5vFxL4UZr3dzJPL8Jg9CCyp3Il1F8fK_o3LqTKDRB3mCYadmcaZhoagZ1Hk=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
