<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48479537' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/04/meta-steals-a-tactic-from-tesla-and-builds-data-centers-in-tents/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta steals a tactic from Tesla and builds data centers in tents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 借鉴特斯拉，在帐篷中搭建数据中心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48479537'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta reportedly borrows a tent-based data center approach from Tesla to cut capex and accelerate deployment, signaling a shift toward modular, temporary infrastructure at scale. The approach raises questions about long-term reliability, cooling, and maintenance in hyperscale deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 据称借鉴特斯拉的帐篷式数据中心思路，旨在降低资本支出、加速部署，体现出在大规模部署中对模块化、临时基础设施的转向。此举也引发对长期可靠性、冷却与维护成本的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/meta-ai-icon-smartphone.jpg?resize=1200,800' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/meta-ai-icon-smartphone.jpg?resize=1200,800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48470578' data-tags='Gaming,Hardware'>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48503009' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theconsensus.dev/p/2026/06/06/python-3-14-garbage-collection-rigamarole.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Python 3.14 garbage collection rigamarole</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python 3.14 垃圾回收的混乱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48503009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48503009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48503009'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An in-depth look at the changes and tensions around Python 3.14&#x27;s garbage collection, explaining how the new heuristics impact pause times and memory profiles. It provides practical guidance for tuning performance-sensitive apps and highlights why GC behavior matters for long-running services. The piece helps developers decide when to tighten or loosen GC settings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入解读 Python 3.14 垃圾回收的新特性与争议，说明新启发式对暂停时间和内存形态的影响。给出针对性能敏感应用的调优建议，并强调 GC 行为对长期运行服务的重要性。帮助开发者判断何时收紧或放宽 GC 设置。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.com/www.politico.eu/article/new-privacy-frontier-europe-eyes-crackdown-smart-glasses/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New privacy frontier: Europe eyes crackdown on smart glasses</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48506324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48506324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48506324'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European policymakers are considering stricter privacy rules for smart glasses, addressing recording, consent, and data handling. The move could shape the adoption and design of AR wearables across Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟监管机构正考虑对智能眼镜实施更严格的隐私规则，涵盖录像、同意与数据处理等方面，可能影响欧洲地区增强现实设备的普及与设计。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48450694' data-tags='Web,Privacy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48450694' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://inavoyage.blogspot.com/2026/06/im-building-parallel-internet-and-its.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m building a parallel internet, and it&#x27;s called The Thinnernet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在构建一个平行互联网，名为 The Thinnernet</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48450694'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Thinnernet is an effort to build a lightweight, parallel internet focused on resilience, privacy, and decentralization—likely using mesh, p2p, and minimal protocols to bypass centralized infrastructure. The project emphasizes small-footprint services and user control, aiming to work in constrained or censored environments. It’s part of a broader wave of experiments exploring alternatives to today&#x27;s centralized web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>The Thinnernet 旨在构建一个轻量的平行互联网，强调弹性、隐私与去中心化，可能采用网格、点对点和精简协议以绕过集中式基础设施。该项目注重小体量服务与用户控制，适用于受限或受审查的环境。它属于探索当今集中化网络替代方案的实验浪潮。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/a/AVvXsEh6x-X2Kjvpr1I7S4COADE-lq87xg3w-txwJ8EvYT8qvRF9RavaID0hZQkgkzPkGN4dBJGL8QSqG0CsOSuOgAJZ14tvPZQKoaMblfZgc1kY2GZ_nplMCGl1PDu8lFYD04tzfhJiLdmDisq2Ad1gzHUEa-1FbFpgAPiYsPsKrYMgG5xVSBsO5RrZyS12_L-R=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/a/AVvXsEh6x-X2Kjvpr1I7S4COADE-lq87xg3w-txwJ8EvYT8qvRF9RavaID0hZQkgkzPkGN4dBJGL8QSqG0CsOSuOgAJZ14tvPZQKoaMblfZgc1kY2GZ_nplMCGl1PDu8lFYD04tzfhJiLdmDisq2Ad1gzHUEa-1FbFpgAPiYsPsKrYMgG5xVSBsO5RrZyS12_L-R=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/joske/yserver' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Yserver: A modern X11 server written in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Yserver：用 Rust 编写的现代 X11 服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48531394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48531394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48531394'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yserver is a modern X11 server implemented in Rust, aiming for safer memory management and modern API design while staying compatible with X11 clients. It signals interest in rethinking legacy windowing stacks with safer systems languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yserver 是一个用 Rust 编写的现代 X11 服务器，旨在实现更安全的内存管理和现代 API，同时保持对 X11 客户端的向后兼容。它体现了用更安全的系统语言重塑传统窗口系统的尝试。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f71da02b98e762d66f8b85dbac37574b37c98c1794c56a4375c3abca2513c13e/joske/yserver' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f71da02b98e762d66f8b85dbac37574b37c98c1794c56a4375c3abca2513c13e/joske/yserver' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(315) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tectonic-typesetting.github.io/en-US/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tectonic: A modernized, complete, self-contained TeX/LaTeX engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tectonic：一个现代化、完整、自包含的 TeX/LaTeX 引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48467697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48467697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48467697'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tectonic is a self-contained TeX/LaTeX engine designed to simplify document typesetting with minimal external dependencies. It integrates fonts and tools, aiming for reliable builds, faster startup, and improved reproducibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tectonic 是一个自包含的 TeX/LaTeX 引擎，旨在通过最小化外部依赖来简化排版工作。它集成字体与工具，追求更可靠的构建、加快启动并提升可重复性。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48437305'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>My automated doubt development process describes a workflow that surfaces or generates doubts during reasoning to curb bias and improve decisions. It outlines how automation can replay scenarios, challenge assumptions, and log doubts for later review. A meta-tool for more disciplined thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者描述了一种自动化过程，用于在推理中产出或揭示怀疑点，以抵消偏见并提升决策质量。文中可能讲解如何通过自动化重现情景、挑战假设，并将疑问记录以便复盘。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48491214' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48491214' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theatlantic.com/technology/2026/06/car-headlights-too-bright-adaptive-beams/687488/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Car headlights don&#x27;t have to be this blinding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>汽车大灯不必如此刺眼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48491214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48491214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48491214'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates for better headlight design with adaptive beams to reduce glare for oncoming drivers, improving nighttime safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>倡导采用自适应光束的更好大灯设计，以减少对向驾驶员眩光、提升夜间行车安全。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48523203'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FDA approves a novel UV-filter ingredient and outlines four key implications: broader protection, safety profile, environmental considerations, and labeling/usage guidance. It situates the ingredient within evolving sunscreen formulations and regulatory processes. Practical notes for consumers and product developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FDA 批准了一种新的防晒成分，并给出四点关键影响：更广的防护、安全性、环境因素与标签/使用指南。将该成分放在不断演进的防晒配方与监管框架中进行解读。为消费者与产品开发者提供实用要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48384525' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48384525' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ydb.tech/do-we-fear-the-serializable-isolation-level-more-than-we-fear-subtle-bugs-5a025401b609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do we fear the serializable isolation level more than we fear subtle bugs (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们是否更担心可串行化隔离级别，而非微妙的错误？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48384525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48384525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48384525'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Serializable isolation promises correctness but often adds complexity and performance costs that may cause more pain than the rare, subtle bugs it aims to prevent. The piece advocates pragmatic cross-checks, selective isolation, and robust testing to balance safety with practicality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>可串行化隔离级别承诺正确性，但往往带来额外的复杂性与性能成本，反而让少数罕见的微妙错误带来更大痛点。文中倡导务实的并发检测、选择性隔离与充分测试，以实现安全性与实际可行性的平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:780/1*keS37tRigV2DLMy2itYZGQ.png' data-full='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:780/1*keS37tRigV2DLMy2itYZGQ.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tribblix resurrects Illumos as a usable, retro-styled distribution, blending OpenSolaris heritage with modern package management. It&#x27;s a reminder of how alternative OS ecosystems keep historical ideas alive and accessible.</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文批评 Claude 的使用体验与输出，分析对齐约束、安全提示及其导致的失败模式。指出在看起来能力强大时，设计选择如何降低实用性，并提出改进建议。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Encrypted Spaces outlines an architecture for collaborative apps centered on client-side privacy and secure data sharing, enabling real-time collaboration with strong privacy guarantees. It discusses data ownership, access control, and the tricky balance of trust in multi-user environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Encrypted Spaces 提出一个面向协作应用的隐私优先架构，强调客户端加密与安全数据共享，支持实时协作。文中讨论了数据所有权、访问控制，以及在多用户环境中构建信任边界的设计要点。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48523752'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic demonstrates how Claude can be steered to assist with chemistry tasks, from reaction planning to data analysis, highlighting domain-specific prompting and safety considerations. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 展示了如何将 Claude 引导到化学任务中，从反应规划到数据分析，强调领域特定提示与安全考量。此举体现了 AI 在专业学科中的推理能力提升，同时也引发对科学工作流中工具链与可靠性的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>租金管制引发的住房财富再分配</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48523773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48523773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48523773'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study quantifies how rent control shifts housing wealth among tenants, landlords, and communities, revealing trade-offs between affordability and property value distribution. It discusses mechanisms like rent ceilings, property investment incentives, and neighborhood effects. The PDF provides data-driven insights for policy design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究量化租金管制如何在租户、房东与社区之间重新分配住房财富，揭示可负担性与房产价值分布之间的权衡。讨论租金上限、房产投资激励与社区效应等机制，PDF 提供基于数据的政策设计见解。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48475898' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(342) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.marginallycompelling.com/p/oh-good-screwworms-are-back' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oh good, screwworms are back (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>哦，好消息，蠕螬虫又回来了（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48475898'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Screwworm outbreaks reappear in 2025, highlighting ongoing pest control and biosecurity challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年蠕螬虫再次出现，凸显持续的病虫害防控与生物安全挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48476202' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48476202' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/entGriff/ezra' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Lightweight Task queue on Erlang/OTP, SQLite-backed, no overengineering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：基于 Erlang/OTP 的轻量级任务队列，SQLite 支持，避免过度设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48476202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48476202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48476202'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN introduces Ezra, a lightweight Erlang/OTP task queue backed by SQLite designed to stay minimal and pragmatic. It emphasizes avoiding overengineering while providing reliable async work handling in small projects. The repository serves as a simple alternative to heavier queues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 介绍了名为 Ezra 的轻量级任务队列，基于 Erlang/OTP，使用 SQLite 作为后端，追求简约实用。强调避免过度设计，适合小型项目的可靠异步任务处理。此实现为寻求简单、可维护队列的开发者提供可落地的替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ad218b2d9f63aa06f329229543c97c9dabaa504c055083439f25e45843bbee10/entGriff/ezra' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ad218b2d9f63aa06f329229543c97c9dabaa504c055083439f25e45843bbee10/entGriff/ezra' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48481524'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Abundance Illusion questions the belief in limitless abundance, exploring limits in resources, governance, and market concentration that temper optimistic narratives around technology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>丰足幻觉质疑对无穷丰足的乐观预期，探讨资源、治理与市场集中等因素如何抑制对技术的过度乐观。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48521236'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trace is a Mac app that records and transcribes meetings offline, with an in-call flagging feature to mark important moments for later review. This offline-first approach reduces data exposure and enables fast, local search without cloud processing—useful for privacy-conscious teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Trace 是一款在 Mac 上离线记录并转写会议的应用，通话中就可标记关键时刻便于日后回顾。离线优先的设计降低数据暴露风险，并支持本地快速搜索，适合注重隐私的团队。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://traceapp.info/og/landing.png' data-full='https://traceapp.info/og/landing.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48524084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48524084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48524084'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Martin Fowler&#x27;s Software Architecture Guide compiles timeless patterns and decisions shaping system quality attributes, from layering to microservices, with pragmatic trade-offs. It offers anchored explanations, anti-patterns, and checklists for architecture evaluation. A reference for engineers designing scalable, maintainable software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Martin Fowler 的软件架构指南汇集影响系统质量属性的经典模式和抉择，从分层到微服务，并给出务实的权衡。提供带锚点的解释、反模式与评估清单，帮助评估架构设计。是设计可扩展、可维护软件的参考。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48443135' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48443135' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(361) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://safedep.io/config-files-that-run-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Config Files That Run Code: Supply Chain Security Blindspot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>会执行代码的配置文件：供应链安全盲点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48443135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48443135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48443135'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Config files that run code create a security blindspot in software supply chains, because they allow executing code during builds or deployments. The piece argues for guardrails like signing, access controls, and reproducible environments to mitigate risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>会执行代码的配置文件构成供应链安全的盲点，因为它们可能在构建或部署阶段执行未受信代码。文章倡导通过签名、访问控制与可重复的环境等措施来降低风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://safedep.io/images/config-files-supply-chain-security-blindspot.jpg' data-full='https://safedep.io/images/config-files-supply-chain-security-blindspot.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://borretti.me/article/human-routers-of-machine-words' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Human Routers of Machine Words</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人类是机器词汇的路由器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48521709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48521709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48521709'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that humans currently route and curate machine-generated language in real time, serving as intermediaries between AI models and end users. It examines prompts, oversight, and the social-technical layer enabling reliable AI communication. The piece prompts reflection on responsibility and control in AI-mediated language.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为人类当前在AI生成内容与终端用户之间充当路由者与编目者，实时把控机器输出的语言。讨论提示设计、监督机制以及实现可靠AI沟通的社会-技术层。引发对 AI 语言交互中的责任与控制的思考。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48519519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48519519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48519519'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PwC finds AI-driven processes can raise administrative costs and claims complexity, potentially driving higher medical bills. The finding prompts a closer look at governance and design in health-tech workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>普华永道指出，AI 驱动的流程可能增加管理成本和理赔复杂度，进而推高医疗账单，提示需要审视健康科技工作流的治理与设计。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48479652' data-tags='Programming,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48479652' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(366) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rvaiya/keyd' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A key remapping daemon for Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于 Linux 的按键重映射守护进程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48479652'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Keyd is a low-level Linux daemon that remaps keys and creates advanced keyboard macros, useful for ergonomic setups and gaming. It runs in user space, offering predictable latency and flexibility for customizing input across X11/Wayland.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Keyd 是一个 Linux 上的底层守护进程，用于重新映射按键并创建高级键盘宏，适合人体工学和游戏等场景。它在用户空间运行，提供可预测的延迟和跨 X11/Wayland 的自定义输入能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/cfaa413abbb16d8be460434972754cadf0f5120877455e20189e694b75518837/rvaiya/keyd' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/cfaa413abbb16d8be460434972754cadf0f5120877455e20189e694b75518837/rvaiya/keyd' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48513222' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48513222' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://6it.dev/blog/on-cpu-physics-and-cpu-cycles-80730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>On CPU Physics and CPU Cycles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于 CPU 物理和 CPU 时钟周期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48513222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48513222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48513222'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CPU-based physics is bounded by instruction throughput and clock cycles, making real-time simulations a tight optimization problem. The author argues for careful scheduling, batching, and avoiding unnecessary branches to keep physics steps within frame budgets. The discussion helps developers rethink where CPU time goes in physics workloads and how to design for performance from the ground up.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为基于 CPU 的物理仿真受限于时钟周期和指令吞吐量。给出提升实时性的实际策略，如改进调度、批处理更新、减少分支等。讨论促使开发者从根本上重新审视物理计算在 CPU 上的成本分配与性能设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://6it.dev/assets/1.a16c5b00.svg' data-full='https://6it.dev/assets/1.a16c5b00.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48461311' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unified Controllable and Faithful Text-to-CAD Generation with LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 LLMs 实现可控且可信的文本到 CAD 生成：统一方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48461311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48461311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48461311'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers present a methodology to produce controllable, faithful CAD geometries from natural language prompts using LLMs, integrating constraint handling and verification. The approach aims to make AI-assisted design more reliable for engineering workflows, reducing iteration time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者提出一种基于大型语言模型的、可控且高保真文本到 CAD 生成的统一方法，将约束与校验嵌入生成过程。该思路有望通过把自然语言提示直接转化为 CAD 模型，提升工程设计的可靠性和效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danielmangum.com/posts/fooling-go-x509-certificate-verification/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fooling Go&#x27;s X.509 Certificate Verification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欺骗 Go 的 X.509 证书验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48425166'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical write-up demonstrates ways to bypass or confuse Go&#x27;s X.509 certificate verification, uncovering edge cases in certificate parsing and validation logic. The findings expose how subtle implementation details can undermine TLS trust, with concrete recommendations for patches and safer libraries. This matters for anyone relying on Go for secure networking—especially servers and clients handling diverse certificate sources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>技术文章展示了如何利用 Go 的 X.509 证书验证中的边界情况进行绕过或迷惑验证流程，揭示了证书解析与校验逻辑中的细微问题。文中给出具体修补建议并推荐更安全的库实现。对依赖 Go 进行安全网络通信的服务端与客户端尤其重要。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

This matters for how apps compile across languages and runtime environments without friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WASM 组件模型正向着 1.0 迈进，强调可组合模块和跨语言边界的互操作性。讨论概述了实现真正组件互操作性所需的标准化要点，以及工具、打包与生命周期管理将如何演进。这对于实现跨语言和运行时环境的无缝应用编译至关重要。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/judge-learns-lawyers-on-both-sides-of-case-used-ai-cancels-trial-kicks-everyone-off-the-case/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Judge Learns Both Sides Used AI, Cancels Trial, Kicks Everyone Off the Case</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法官获悉双方均使用 AI，取消审判并将相关人员除名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48462428'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A judge cancels a trial after discovering both parties used AI in their filings or prep, and removes all participants from the case to avoid AI-related prejudice. It underscores the legal risks of AI-assisted practice and the need for clear guidelines on AI usage in court.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法官在发现双方都使用 AI 助力后取消审判并将所有参与者逐出法院，以避免 AI 引发的偏见。这件事凸显了 AI 助力在司法领域的风险，以及对在法庭中使用 AI 的明确规范的需要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/tingey-injury-law-firm-DZpc4UY8ZtY-unsplash.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/tingey-injury-law-firm-DZpc4UY8ZtY-unsplash.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48458636' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dfarq.homeip.net/eagle-computer-the-rise-and-fall-of-an-early-pc-clone/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Eagle Computer: The rise and fall of an early PC clone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Eagle Computer：早期 PC 克隆的兴衰</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48458636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48458636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48458636'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Eagle Computer captures the rise and fall of an early PC clone, illustrating how competing hardware ecosystems formed around IBM PC compatibility, and why some startups thrived while others faded. The story is a window into the hardware-era dynamics and business models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾 Eagle Computer 在早期 PC 克隆浪潮中的崛起与坠落，揭示了以 IBM PC 兼容性为核心的硬件生态如何成形，以及为何有些创业公司得以兴盛、另一些则黯淡。通过这一段历史窥见硬件时代的商业模式与竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/eagle-computer-social.jpg?fit=964%2C542&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/eagle-computer-social.jpg?fit=964%2C542&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(390) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vanyapr/makaroshki' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Macaroni – a single HTML file messenger</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48486944' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48486944' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48486944'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact, self-contained messenger implemented as a single HTML file, showcasing how to bundle UI, logic, and data into one portable artifact. It&#x27;s a minimalist take on a chat app designed for easy distribution and offline use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个紧凑且自包容的消息应用，用单一 HTML 文件实现，展示了如何把界面、逻辑和数据打包成一个便携的产物。体现了便于分发与离线使用的极简聊天应用思路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(391) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mgunlogson.github.io/magma/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: 3D print Z reinforcement via injected loops</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48460762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48460762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48460762'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: a low-cost technique to reinforce the Z-axis in 3D prints by injecting loops into the print path. The approach reduces wobble and improves rigidity, and is accessible to hobbyists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：通过在打印中注入环来强化 Z 轴，成本低且易于实施，能减少晃动、提高刚性，适合爱好者尝试。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(392) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chipsandcheese.com/p/an-interview-with-intels-kira-boyko' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Interview with Intel&#x27;s Kira Boyko: Xeon 6&#x27;s Product Director</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interview with Intel&#x27;s Kira Boyko, product director for Xeon 6, covering roadmap priorities, architecture considerations, and how the platform aims to balance AI workloads with traditional server applications. The talk provides insight into Intel&#x27;s strategy for data-center CPUs amid AI acceleration trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对英特尔 Xeon 6 的产品总监 Kira Boyko 的采访，讨论了路线图优先级、架构考量，以及该平台在平衡 AI 工作负载与传统服务器应用方面的定位。为了解英特尔在 AI 加速浪潮中的数据中心 CPU 策略提供了洞见。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48516558' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/business/2026/jun/12/sam-bankman-fried-loses-appeal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sam Bankman-Fried loses bid to appeal against fraud conviction in FTX case</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>萨姆·班克曼-弗里德就 FTX 案欺诈定罪上诉未获通过</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48516558'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The appeal against the fraud conviction in the FTX case was denied, keeping the conviction in place. The decision reinforces accountability for high-profile crypto-related fraud and may influence ongoing legal scrutiny of the industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于 FTX 案件的欺诈定罪的上诉被法院驳回，维持原判。这一裁决强化了对高知名度 加密货币相关欺诈行为的问责，并可能影响行业的后续法律审查。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/e5b3cc98f314ea4639900d8e28eb61ac8ff65b3c/640_0_5789_4629/master/5789.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=600cf5c6da2c06b59d873381333f920e' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/e5b3cc98f314ea4639900d8e28eb61ac8ff65b3c/640_0_5789_4629/master/5789.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=600cf5c6da2c06b59d873381333f920e' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48531449' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tumbleweed.nu/r/lm-3/uv/amber.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chaosnet (1981)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chaosnet（1981年）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48531449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48531449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48531449'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chaosnet was an early campus network and a before-TCP/IP experiment in network design. This 1981 lookback highlights its protocol choices, goals, and why such projects shaped how researchers thought about interoperability and distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chaosnet 是早期校园网络的一次 TCP/IP 之前的实验。本文回顾 1981 年的设计要点、目标及其如何影响研究者对互联互通与分布式系统的思考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以轻松对话的形式向六岁孩子讲解函数式编程，聚焦不可变性、纯函数和高阶函数等要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48463273' data-tags='Startups,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48463273' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48463273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Transload (YC P26) – Measuring freight items with CCTV</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN: Transload（YC 第26期）— 用 CCTV 测量货物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48463273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48463273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48463273'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Transload uses CCTV footage and computer vision to measure freight items, enabling automated, scalable logistics analytics. Backed by YC, the startup aims to reduce manual inspection and accelerate shipping workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Transload 通过闭路电视画面与计算机视觉实现货物尺寸和数量的自动测量，提供可扩展的物流分析。该 YC 支持的初创公司旨在减少人工检查并加快运输流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-01581-x' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A whale necropolis has been found</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>鲸鱼墓地被发现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48481546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48481546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48481546'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paleontologists report a whale necropolis, a mass death site that preserves insights into ancient marine ecosystems and social behavior. The discovery enriches understanding of species lifecycles and fossil preservation. It also demonstrates how unusual fossil assemblages can reveal ecosystem dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>古生物学家报道发现鲸鱼墓地——一个大量死亡的遗骸聚集区，揭示了古代海洋生态系统与社会行为的线索。该发现丰富了对物种生命周期与化石保存的理解，也展示了非常规化石组合如何揭示生态系统动态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-01581-x/d41586-026-01581-x_52477224.jpg' data-full='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-01581-x/d41586-026-01581-x_52477224.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zinnia is a modular, 64-bit Unix-like kernel written in Rust, focusing on safety and modularity. It&#x27;s an ambitious project that demonstrates Rust’s viability for operating-system kernels and could influence future OS design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zinnia 是用 Rust 编写的模块化、64 位类 Unix 内核，关注安全性与模块化。该项目展示了 Rust 在操作系统内核中的可行性，或影响未来的系统设计。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(408) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discourse.haskell.org/t/h2jvm-a-haskell-library-for-writing-jvm-bytecode/14182' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>H2JVM – A Haskell Library for Writing JVM Bytecode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>H2JVM：用于编写 JVM 字节码的 Haskell 库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48433356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48433356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48433356'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>H2JVM offers a Haskell library for programmatically emitting JVM bytecode, enabling developers to generate Java-compatible code with type safety from Haskell. It opens up opportunities for tooling, language experimentation, and JVM-based pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>H2JVM 提供一个用 Haskell 编写的 JVM 字节码生成库，使开发者能够从 Haskell 高效地生成 Java 字节码，具备类型安全。这为工具链构建、语言实验与基于 JVM 的工作流程带来新的可能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(410) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ataraxy-labs.github.io/weave/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Weave: Merging based on language structure and not lines</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Weave：基于语言结构的合并</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48523705' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48523705' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48523705'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Weave proposes a code-merge approach driven by syntax trees and language structure rather than line-based diffs. It argues this yields more semantic merges and fewer conflicts, especially for multi-language repos. The project includes a prototype and discuss tradeoffs with traditional VCS merging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Weave 提出一种以语法树与语言结构为驱动的代码合并方法，而非逐行 diff。认为这样的合并能产生更语义化的结果、减少冲突，尤其适用于多语言代码库。文章还给出原型实现及与传统版本控制合并的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这条推文在探讨 agents.md 文件是否有助于编写编码代理，可能关注结构化文档在提升自动化、可重现性和工具协作中的作用。核心观点是，良好且机器可读的指南有助于减少错位并提升工具编排效果。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(413) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2021/01/the-unreasonable-effectiveness-of-simple-html/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The unreasonable effectiveness of simple HTML</a></p>

The piece argues against overengineering and showcases how a minimal HTML baseline can deliver fast, resilient user experiences with smaller surface areas for bugs and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纯 HTML 在性能、可访问性和可靠性方面往往优于重前端框架，尤其适用于以内容为中心的应用。文章主张避免过度设计，展示以最小的 HTML 基线实现快速、稳健体验的价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lisyarus.github.io/blog/posts/quivers-a-year-of-linear-algebra-by-drawing-arrows.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quivers: A year of linear algebra by drawing arrows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Quivers：用画箭头来学习线性代数的一年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48474982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48474982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48474982'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quivers uses arrows and directed graphs to visualize linear algebra concepts over a year-long exploration. The approach aims to build intuition for vector spaces, linear maps, and transformations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过用箭头和有向图来直观呈现线性代数概念，这是一年中的学习尝试。该方法帮助理解向量空间、线性映射与变换。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48405890' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://groups.csail.mit.edu/medg/people/psz/Licklider.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Man-Computer Symbiosis J. C. R. Licklider (1960)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人–机共生：J. C. R. 里克莱德（1960）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48405890'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>J. C. R. Licklider&#x27;s 1960 essay argued for a partnership between humans and computers, envisioning cooperative problem-solving and cumulative intelligence through networks. The concept underpins modern HCI and AI systems, shaping how we design interactive tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1960 年，洛克利德提出了人机共生的设想，强调人和计算机携手协作、通过网络实现协同智能。该观点奠定了现代人机交互与人工智能系统的设计思路，影响至今。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48462184' data-tags='Science,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48462184' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using Optical Aberrations to Distinguish Real Astronomical Transients</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>利用光学像差区分真实天文瞬变</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48462184'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Using optical aberrations as a diagnostic, the work shows how distortions in telescope imaging can help distinguish real astronomical transients from artifacts. The method can improve the reliability of transient catalogs and observations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明利用光学像差可以区分真实的天文瞬变与观测伪影，从而提升瞬变事件目录的可靠性与观测质量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(432) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ox.ac.uk/news/2026-06-09-ancient-genome-duplications-laid-the-foundations-of-complex-brains' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ancient genome duplications laid the foundations of complex brains</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>远古基因组重复奠定了复杂大脑的基础</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48522310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48522310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48522310'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research links ancient whole-genome duplications to the emergence of complex brain structures, suggesting early vertebrates leveraged gene duplications to diversify neural circuits. The findings illuminate long-standing questions about brain evolution and may influence how we study genomic architectures in the future.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究将远古的全基因组重复与大脑结构复杂性的出现联系起来，提示早期脊椎动物通过基因重复来扩展神经回路。研究结果揭示大脑进化的长期问题，或影响未来对基因组结构的研究方向。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(433) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cloud.google.com/blog/products/data-analytics/how-the-open-knowledge-format-can-improve-data-sharing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google proposes Open Knowledge Format based on Markdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌提出基于 Markdown 的开放知识格式</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google proposes an Open Knowledge Format based on Markdown to improve data sharing and interoperability across tools and systems. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌提出基于 Markdown 的开放知识格式，旨在改善工具与系统之间的数据共享与互操作性。该倡议推动采用轻量、可人类编辑的标准，以简化知识图谱、文档与数据管线的工作流。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Transformer 注意力中的执行控制不足</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48484282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48484282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48484282'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a new study, transformer attention shows weak executive control, suggesting models struggle with long-term planning and resource management. The finding hints at limitations of current attention mechanisms and potential directions for improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究发现，变压器模型的注意力存在执行控制不足的问题，表明模型在长期规划与资源管理方面存在局限。该结果揭示了现有注意力机制的局限性，并指向改进方向。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有时过早优化也很有趣。文章认为在已知瓶颈或需要快速迭代以关注性能的场景中，提前进行微优化可能带来实际收益，并提供了具体案例与平衡视角。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48397986' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48397986' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(444) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wxwidgets.org/blog/2024/01/using-xdg-compliant-config-files/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using XDG-Compliant Config Files (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用符合 XDG 规范的配置文件（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48397986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48397986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48397986'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This guide explains how to adopt the XDG Base Directory specification for storing config, cache, and data files on Unix-like systems to avoid scattering files across home directories. It covers practical path choices, migration tips, and cross-platform considerations to make applications behave well on Linux and macOS. Following XDG conventions reduces clutter, improves portability, and makes system-wide management easier for users and package maintainers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇指南说明了如何在类 Unix 系统中采用 XDG 基础目录规范来存放配置、缓存和数据文件，避免将文件散落在 home 目录。内容涵盖路径选择、迁移建议以及跨平台注意点，帮助应用在 Linux 与 macOS 上表现良好。遵循 XDG 规范可减少杂乱、提升可移植性并方便系统与包管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wxwidgets.org/assets/img/header-logo.png' data-full='https://wxwidgets.org/assets/img/header-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48419769' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(445) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://writings.stephenwolfram.com/2026/06/games-between-programs-the-ruliology-of-competition/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Games Between Programs: The Ruliology of Competition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>程序之间的博弈：竞争的律理学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48419769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48419769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48419769'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stephen Wolfram explores games between programs, offering a lens on how rule-based interactions produce competitive dynamics. The piece connects computational principles to strategic behavior in software and AI contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>沃尔夫拉姆探讨程序之间的博弈，揭示基于规则的互动如何产生竞争性动力。将计算原理与软件和人工智能场景中的策略行为联系起来。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://content.wolfram.com/sites/43/2026/06/gbp-tweet-hero-v3-FINAL.png' data-full='https://content.wolfram.com/sites/43/2026/06/gbp-tweet-hero-v3-FINAL.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48451404' data-tags='Hardware,Startups,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48451404' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(446) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/06/08/donut-lab-solid-state-battery-exposed-lithium-ion-fraud/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Donut Lab&#x27;s &#x27;solid-state&#x27; battery exposed as regular li-ion in investigation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>调查揭露 Donut Lab 所称“固态”电池实为普通锂离子电池</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48451404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48451404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48451404'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An investigation found that Donut Lab&#x27;s touted &#x27;solid-state&#x27; battery cells were actually conventional lithium-ion cells, raising allegations of misrepresentation and eroding credibility. The revelation prompts scrutiny of startup claims in battery tech, where demonstrations can be misleading and independent verification is crucial. Investors and partners will likely demand clearer evidence and testing protocols before backing similar promises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查发现 Donut Lab 所宣称的“固态”电池并非真正固态，而是常规锂离子电池，涉嫌夸大与误导，严重损害了信誉。此事让电池技术领域的创业宣传受到更严格审视，表明演示可能存在误导，必须依赖独立验证。投资者与合作方很可能在支持类似承诺前要求更明确的证据与测试流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/02/Donut-Lab-solid-state-battery.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/02/Donut-Lab-solid-state-battery.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(447) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.notus.org/policy/irs-tax-processing-detail-hr-it-test' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The IRS Moved IT and HR Staff to Process Taxes. It&#x27;s Not Going Well</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IRS 将 IT 与 HR 员工调往税务处理任务，情况不太顺利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48448811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48448811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48448811'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IRS moved IT and HR staff to tax processing tasks, leading to outages and inefficiencies. The situation highlights the risks of reorganizing government IT around mission-critical processes and the need for stable, purpose-built systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IRS 将 IT 与 HR 员工调往税务处理任务，导致系统故障与效率下降。这凸显政府 IT 项目在资源重新配置中的风险，以及对稳定、专用系统的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.notus.org/dims4/default/51d5892/2147483647/strip/true/crop/8640x4860+0+0/resize/1440x810!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fk2-prod-aji.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com%2Fbrightspot%2Fa2%2F4a%2Fa988397945828f9f9b8cc43698ae%2Fap26082795472233.jpg' data-full='https://static.notus.org/dims4/default/51d5892/2147483647/strip/true/crop/8640x4860+0+0/resize/1440x810!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fk2-prod-aji.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com%2Fbrightspot%2Fa2%2F4a%2Fa988397945828f9f9b8cc43698ae%2Fap26082795472233.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(449) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://replicated.wiki/blog/uris.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Beagle: Git, URIs and all the dirty words</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Beagle：Git、URI 与那些“脏话”</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(451) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mastermariners.org.au/stories-from-the-past/6481-the-world-s-largest-floating-dry-dock-was-towed-across-the-atlantic-to-bermuda-in-1869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Giant Floating Victorian Drydock</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>世界上最大的漂浮式干船坞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48425413'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Historic engineering feat: the world’s largest floating dry dock was towed across the Atlantic to Bermuda in 1869, illustrating 19th-century maritime ingenuity and the scale of industrial-age ships&#x27; maintenance. The tale blends technology, logistics, and regional history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>史上最大的漂浮式干船坞在1869年横跨大西洋被拖往百慕大，展现19世纪海运工程与造船维护能力的规模与胆识。这段历史兼具技术、物流与区域史的交织。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48459999' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(452) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nibblestew.blogspot.com/2026/06/faking-keyword-arguments-to-functions.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Faking keyword arguments to functions in C++</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 C++ 中伪造关键字参数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48459999' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48459999' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48459999'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores techniques to mimic keyword arguments in C++, using builder patterns, named parameter idioms, or templated wrappers to improve readability and configurability without sacrificing type safety. It highlights trade-offs for large codebases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在 C++ 中伪造关键字参数的技术路径，包括构建器模式、命名参数习惯和模板包装，以在不牺牲类型安全的前提下提升可读性与配置性，并分析在大型代码库中的取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEipxolHgFLDFeecuGLIStC9PHsYEy2ORmWmYmGLW3OAR562zUbR5Yl3RtBcdwHDCrPB7gUBBQUzVR-GrRJr4ZTzbGiLoRXUzYKa_KIzL0qnCsY9_DZOTvlW92oJKv0P6RWx4gl1vkUzGtBOX_JYK2-L3SyXoBrDwypMLjG24g8N8rtQQngNvcLiPdoJ_Bs/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/python_kwargs.png' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEipxolHgFLDFeecuGLIStC9PHsYEy2ORmWmYmGLW3OAR562zUbR5Yl3RtBcdwHDCrPB7gUBBQUzVR-GrRJr4ZTzbGiLoRXUzYKa_KIzL0qnCsY9_DZOTvlW92oJKv0P6RWx4gl1vkUzGtBOX_JYK2-L3SyXoBrDwypMLjG24g8N8rtQQngNvcLiPdoJ_Bs/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/python_kwargs.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48444654' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(453) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bernsteinbear.com/blog/value-numbering/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Value Numbering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>值编号</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48444654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48444654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48444654'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Value numbering, a compiler optimization, aims to identify equivalent expressions to avoid redundant calculations. The post explains how it works and its practical limits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>值编号是一种编译器优化技术，旨在识别等价表达式以避免重复计算。文中解释了工作原理及其在实际中的局限性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(454) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/react/react/pull/36173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Port React Compiler to Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将 React 编译器移植到 Rust</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48473662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48473662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48473662'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pull request proposes porting the React compiler to Rust, aiming for faster builds and memory safety. If adopted, the move could influence how critical tooling in the React ecosystem evolves, potentially accelerating compile times for large apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 PR 提议将 React 编译器移植到 Rust，目标是提升编译速度并增强内存安全。若被采纳，这一做法可能影响 React 生态中关键工具链的演化，帮助大型应用的构建时间更短。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48527946' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48527946' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(455) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ponytail – make your AI agent think like the laziest senior dev in the room</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ponytail：让 AI 助手像最懒的资深开发者一样思考</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48527946'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ponytail is a tool to steer AI agents toward the pragmatic, low-effort reasoning style of a senior developer, emphasizing efficient prompts and sensible defaults. It aims to improve reliability and speed of AI-assisted coding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ponytail 是一个工具，帮助 AI 代理模仿资深开发者的务实、低成本思维方式，强调高效提示与合理默认。目标是提升 AI 辅助编码的可靠性与速度。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48508949' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(459) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can I Buy Your KV Cache?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可以购买你的 KV 缓存吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48508949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48508949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48508949'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative research paper examines the economics and security of transformer KV caches, asking whether a model&#x27;s cached key/value states could be bought or sold. The work highlights risks around privacy and intellectual property in hosted ML services and suggests mitigations to prevent leakage or monetization of internal model state.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇研究论文探讨了模型的 transformer KV 缓存是否可能被买卖，揭示托管 AI 服务中潜在的隐私与知识产权风险。这项工作分析了缓存带来的攻击面，并提出防止内部模型状态被泄露或货币化的对策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reddit&#x27;s RSS feeds have begun rate limiting, causing updates to lag. The post outlines practical mitigations, like caching, respecting retry-after headers, or using official APIs with proper authentication.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(467) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/octalide/mach' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Mach – A compiled systems language looking for contributions</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48453666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48453666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48453666'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mach is a compiled systems language seeking community contributions, offering a low-level, performance-focused platform with opportunities for enthusiasts to influence its design and ecosystem. The post invites developers to get involved and shape its future.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mach 是一门面向社区贡献的编译型系统语言，提供低级、注重性能的平台，邀请爱好者参与设计与生态建设，共同塑造未来。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48485160' data-tags='Security,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48485160' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(468) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sethmlarson.dev/are-insecure-code-completions-a-vulnerability' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Are insecure code completions in PyCharm a vulnerability?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PyCharm 的不安全代码补全是否构成漏洞？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48485160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48485160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48485160'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece questions whether code completion suggesting insecure patterns in PyCharm constitutes a vulnerability, weighing potential risks against the benefits of productivity. It highlights the importance of safer defaults and better prompts in AI-assisted IDEs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑 PyCharm 的代码补全在暴露不安全模式时是否构成漏洞，并在生产力收益与安全风险之间做权衡。强调在 AI 辅助的集成开发环境中需要更安全的默认与提示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.com/sethmlarson.png' data-full='https://github.com/sethmlarson.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(469) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gutenberg.org/files/27462/27462-h/27462-h.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Last Evolution, by John W Campbell Jr. (1932)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48478285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48478285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48478285'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 1932 science fiction tale by Campbell Jr., exploring evolution, transcendence, and humanity&#x27;s future; a quintessential golden-age story that influenced later SF.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这部1932年的科幻短篇探讨进化与人类未来，是黄金时代文学的重要作品，对后来的科幻产生了深远影响。</p>
