<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48532178' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48532178' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(470) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/ai-and-ml/2026/06/14/ai-is-code-and-cant-be-prompted-into-being-smarter/5254141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is code – and can&#x27;t be prompted into being smarter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 是代码，不能靠提示变得更聪明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48532178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48532178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48532178'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI systems are software, and prompting alone cannot create real intelligence gains; improvements require better models, data, and training. It warns against over-reliance on clever prompts and encourages a focus on architecture, safety, and compositional capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 AI 是软件，单靠提示无法真正提升智能水平；改进需改进模型、数据和训练方式。警示不要过分依赖“聪明的提示”，应关注架构、安全性及系统性能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/227602.jpg?imageId=227602&amp;x=0&amp;y=11.03&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=88.97&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=11.03&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=88.97&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/227602.jpg?imageId=227602&amp;x=0&amp;y=11.03&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=88.97&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=11.03&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=88.97&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟监管机构正在评估 Anthropic 决定的实际影响，反映出 AI 供应商选择对合规、竞争与市场动态的潜在影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thoughtforms.life/trophic-memory-deer-and-a-truly-unique-scientific-object/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trophic memory, deer, and a unique scientific object</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>营养记忆、鹿群与一个独特的科学对象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48468352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48468352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48468352'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how memory operates across trophic levels in deer and introduces a novel scientific object that enables new observations. The piece blends ecology with methodological innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨鹿群在营养级记忆中的作用，并介绍一种新型科学对象以支持观测，融合生态学与方法论创新的思考。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>斯坦福科学家再生丢失的软骨、逆转关节炎</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48518480'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stanford researchers report regenerating damaged cartilage and reversing arthritis in a breakthrough study. If replicated, it could transform treatments for degenerative joint diseases and reduce surgical interventions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯坦福研究人员在突破性研究中再生受损软骨并逆转关节炎，如被验证，可能改变退化性关节疾病的治疗方式，减少外科手术需求。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(474) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://automato.substack.com/p/apple-wwdc-and-the-fable-5-embargo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloud-based LLM gold rush is ending</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>云端大语言模型热潮正在结束</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48527817'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The cloud-based LLM craze is cooling as cost, latency, and governance frictions bite, pushing enterprises toward on-premises or hybrid models and more specialized solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>云端大语言模型热潮正在降温，成本、延迟和治理难题日益凸显，企业转向本地部署或混合方案，以及更专业化的模型。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48485277' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48485277' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fulcrum.inc/2026/06/09/inverse-rubric-optimization.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inverse Rubric Optimization: A testbed for agent science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>逆向评量标准优化：代理科学的测试床</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48485277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48485277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48485277'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inverse Rubric Optimization presents a framework to frame agent behavior in terms of evaluative rubrics that can be inverted to study alignment, incentive design, and evaluation dynamics. It offers a testbed for experimenting with how goals translate into observable actions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>逆向评量标准优化提供了一种将代理行为以评估性准则来框定的框架，并可对准则进行反演研究对齐、激励设计和评估动力学。它为研究目标如何转化为可观察行动提供了测试床。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48504220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48504220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48504220'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oil storage hub in Cushing, Oklahoma, is seeing inventories bottom out, signaling tight supply conditions and potential price dynamics as markets digest storage constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>卡欣储油中心库存降至低位，反映市场储存容量受限，或对油价与区域市场造成影响。文章分析供应与需求、储存瓶颈及价格传导。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48524688' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/jun/14/trial-of-12mph-bike-lane-speed-limit-grinds-gears-of-dutch-cyclists' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trial of 12mph bike lane speed limit grinds gears of Dutch cyclists</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰12英里/小时自行车道限速试点引发骑行者不满</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48524688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48524688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48524688'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Dutch trial of a 12 mph bike-lane speed limit sparked debate among cyclists and policymakers about safety, flow, and rider autonomy in dense urban cycling. It signals how regulatory experiments can reshape lane design and user behavior in Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰对自行车道设定每小时12英里限速的试点引发了骑行者与政策制定者之间关于安全、通行与骑行自主权的讨论。此举或将重塑欧洲城市自行车道设计与使用行为。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/fbfa33337bcd201c96cb4ba57bbe54d5a064504f/464_0_4638_3710/master/4638.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=25106e2e3a0c2bb85dd12a2d9db24111' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/fbfa33337bcd201c96cb4ba57bbe54d5a064504f/464_0_4638_3710/master/4638.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=25106e2e3a0c2bb85dd12a2d9db24111' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48491376' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48491376' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/AnalogCyberNuke/RE-Verse-2026-Slides/blob/main/Reverse26.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>(Re//Verse 2026) Taxonomy and Deobfuscation of a Real World Binary Obfuscator [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RE//Verse 2026：真实世界二进制混淆器的分类与去混淆 [PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48491376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48491376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48491376'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The talk presents a taxonomy of real-world binary obfuscation techniques and practical deobfuscation methods, helping defenders understand how obfuscation hides intent and how to reverse engineer obscured code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该演讲对真实世界二进制混淳技术进行了分类，并给出去混淳的实用方法，帮助安全研究者理解混淆如何隐藏意图并提供系统化的逆向分析思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/46972a246d535601ec9fb33f2386ec4fddff20085d05940544f781190233a8d8/AnalogCyberNuke/RE-Verse-2026-Slides' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/46972a246d535601ec9fb33f2386ec4fddff20085d05940544f781190233a8d8/AnalogCyberNuke/RE-Verse-2026-Slides' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48516672' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48516672' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(479) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/06/10/science/humans-walking-veer-left-counterclockwise.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nearly Everyone, Everywhere, Veers Left When Walking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>几乎所有人在走路时都会向左偏转</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48516672'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds a universal bias where people tend to veer left when walking, suggesting brain lateralization or balance factors influence locomotion. The finding has implications for navigation design, VR experiences, and crowd dynamics, inviting further study into human perception and movement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现人们在走路时普遍偏向左侧，这提示大脑半球偏侧化或平衡因素可能影响步态。该发现对导航设计、虚拟现实体验和人群动力学具有影响，未来可进一步研究人类感知与运动的联系。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(480) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modular.com/blog/democratizing-ai-compute-part-5-what-about-cuda-c-alternatives' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What about OpenCL and CUDA C++ alternatives?</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462577' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462577' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48462577'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece surveys alternatives to CUDA/C++ for AI compute, including OpenCL and other frameworks, weighing portability, performance, and ecosystem trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章评估 CUDA/C++ 的替代方案，如 OpenCL 等在 AI 计算中的可移植性、性能与生态系统的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(481) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.windowscentral.com/microsoft/windows-11/windows-11-users-are-tired-of-microsoft-account-requirements-and-workarounds' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows 11 users are tired of MS account requirements creeping into everything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 11 用户厌倦 Microsoft 账户要求渗透到方方面面</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48533101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48533101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48533101'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Users push back against Microsoft account requirements infiltrating many parts of Windows 11, with workarounds and policy tensions around onboarding and offline usage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用户反感 Microsoft 账户要求渗透到 Windows 11 的方方面面，并寻找变通方案、讨论上线流程与离线使用的策略冲突。文章凸显产品设计与用户自主性之间的持续摩擦。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48528025'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports a measles surge in Utah raising concerns about rollback of measles elimination progress, with outbreaks linked to vaccination gaps and public health resilience.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48514124' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48514124' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(483) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vladignatyev/brain-map-skill' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Skill for your agent to visualize your gbrain and Obsidian</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：让你的代理可视化你的“gbrain”和 Obsidian 的技能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48514124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48514124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48514124'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN project that adds a skill allowing an AI agent to visualize its own reasoning (the &#x27;gbrain&#x27;) and map it into an Obsidian notebook. The tool lowers the barrier to introspection, aiding debugging and collaboration with human teammates. It showcases practical thinking visualization for AI workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 项目新增一个技能：让代理能够可视化自己的推理过程（gbrain），并将结果映射到 Obsidian 中。该工具降低了对齐与调试的门槛，便于与人类团队协作。展示了在 AI 工作流中进行思维可视化的实用路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d43b20ca013442b8cff4c3a3992e1aa817c39cbbe2bb2aaecd4d95595dfd4d59/vladignatyev/brain-map-skill' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d43b20ca013442b8cff4c3a3992e1aa817c39cbbe2bb2aaecd4d95595dfd4d59/vladignatyev/brain-map-skill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48512286' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48512286' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(484) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gist.github.com/alurm/2ca14be134d719fe7431217a6b18d91e' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A generic dynamic array in C that stores no capacity and needs no struct</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个在 C 语言中实现的通用动态数组：不存储容量、也不需要结构体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48512286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48512286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48512286'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clever C technique for a dynamic array that doesn&#x27;t store capacity or require a separate struct, using macro tricks and pointer arithmetic. It demonstrates how to package generic data structures with minimal overhead, at the cost of portability and clarity. The example invites reconsideration of how metadata is managed in low-level code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个在 C 语言中实现的通用动态数组，它既不存储容量也不需要单独的结构体。通过宏和指针运算实现通用性与低开销，便于在对性能要求极高的场景中使用。这一技巧也引发对元数据如何在底层实现中管理的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' data-full='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(485) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/selfhosted/comments/1u4wqnj/psa_oracle_is_changing_free_tier_limits_update_by/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oracle is changing free tier limits. Update by the 15th to avoid charges</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Oracle 正在收紧免费层限制。请在15日之前更新以避免计费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48530270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48530270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48530270'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oracle is tightening its free tier limits; users should adjust usage or migrate before the cutoff to avoid charges. This affects self-hosters and startups relying on Oracle Cloud&#x27;s freebies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Oracle 正在收紧免费层，需在截止日期前调整使用策略以避免产生费用。依赖 Oracle 云免费额度的自托管与初创公司需重新评估工作负载。</p>
</div>
